### [119] [Grid Integration of Gigawatt-Scale Hydrogen Hubs: A Multi-Timescale Stability Analysis and Connection Requirements for Weak Grid Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17019)
*Mohamed Shamseldein*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本论文评估了大规模绿氢电解中心对电网的影响，发现标准负荷模型不足以准确描述其动态特性，并提出了改进的PERC1模型和标准化并网要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着吉瓦级电解制氢设施的部署，这类基于电力电子接口的新型负荷对电网的谐波、电压稳定性和频率响应带来新挑战，需对其进行系统性评估。

Method: 采用‘全频谱’开源建模方法，结合PandaPower（稳态分析）、ANDES（机电暂态仿真）和ParaEMT（电磁暂态分析）进行多时间尺度仿真。

Result: 发现标准负荷模型（如PERC1）无法准确反映氢工厂的动态行为；提出需修改过程安全锁存机制和重启电压阈值以避免长时间停机风险。

Conclusion: 应针对氢电解枢纽制定专门的并网标准，并改进现有负荷模型结构，以确保大电网的安全稳定运行。

Abstract: The global transition toward green hydrogen is driving the deployment of gigawatt-scale electrolysis centers, introducing a novel, converter-dominated load class to the bulk power system. Unlike conventional industrial loads, these facilities utilize extensive power electronics interfaces with fast dynamics comparable to Inverter-Based Resources (IBRs). This paper presents a comprehensive grid impact assessment of large-scale hydrogen hubs, focusing on harmonic injection, voltage stability in low Short Circuit Ratio (SCR) environments, and frequency response capabilities. Adopting a "full-spectrum" open-source modeling approach, the study utilizes PandaPower for large-scale steady-state contingency assessment; ANDES for electromechanical dynamic simulations to evaluate Fast Frequency Response (FFR); and ParaEMT for high-fidelity electromagnetic transient analysis of harmonic distortion and Low Voltage Ride-Through (LVRT). A critical finding of this study is that standard load models, including the generic PERC1 (data center) model, are insufficient for hydrogen hubs. The paper recommends specific structural modifications to the PERC1 model - specifically regarding process safety latches and restart voltage thresholds - to accurately capture the risk of prolonged plant tripping. Based on these findings, the paper proposes a set of standardized connection requirements to ensure secure integration.

</details>


### [120] [Python-based RTL Generator Demonstrated on a Low-IF 2-FSK Wireless Communication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17042)
*Brandon P. Hippe,David C. Burnett*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Python的可扩展RTL生成器，用于优化单芯片微型节点（SCuM）的数字基带硬件设计，支持多种硬件平台并促进AI辅助设计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低无线通信系统中硬件功耗，尤其是在模拟前端降噪电路中的高功耗问题，需对数字基带硬件进行更紧密的架构协同优化。

Method: 开发了一个与仿真和测试环境紧密结合的、基于Python的可扩展RTL生成器，以实现跨平台（如流片和FPGA）灵活部署，并支持AI辅助设计。

Result: 该方法提高了设计灵活性，支持标准兼容性（如IEEE 802.15.4和蓝牙低功耗），并在不同硬件平台上展现出良好的适应性。

Conclusion: 通过RTL生成器与系统架构的深度集成，可在牺牲部分噪声性能的前提下显著降低功耗，适用于物联网等低功耗应用场景。

Abstract: Hardware optimization is critical in the design of efficient wireless communication systems. Wireless communication hardware often consumes a significant fraction of the total system's power budget, with much of this power used in circuits that reduce various types of noise, particularly in the analog front end. The Single-Chip Micro Mote, or SCuM, uses a crystal-free radio architecture and makes design trade-offs that favor power consumption over noise performance while maintaining standards compatibility with popular Internet-of-Things (IoT) protocols such as IEEE 802.15.4 and Bluetooth Low Energy. In the continued development of SCuM, we recognize that the digital baseband hardware developed can be more closely optimized with the architecture of the chip. In this paper, we present an extensible Python-based RTL generator that is closely linked to simulation and testing environments. This approach provides flexibility for use on different hardware platforms, such as tape-outs and FPGA implementations, and has promise in AI-assisted design workflows.

</details>


### [121] [Physics-informed Reinforcement Learning for Stochastic Reach-Avoid Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17117)
*Hikaru Hoshino,Yorie Nakahira*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息强化学习（PIRL）框架，用于解决高维随机可达-避障分析中的计算难题，结合了PINNs和强化学习的优势，通过分阶段训练策略提高了求解哈密顿-雅可比偏微分方程的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法在高维系统中计算不可行，PINNs易陷入不准确的局部极小值，而强化学习虽可扩展但可能不满足控制方程，因此需要一种更可靠的方法。

Method: 提出一种分阶段PIRL算法：首先使用时序差分actor-critic学习引导critic网络逼近可达-避障价值函数，然后逐步引入PDE残差和边界条件损失以确保与控制方程的一致性。

Result: 该方法缓解了传统PINN的失效问题，达到了与成功训练的PINN相当的精度，并在两个案例研究中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: PIRL框架有效结合了强化学习的可扩展性与物理约束的准确性，为高维随机控制系统的安全分析提供了更具鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Stochastic reach-avoid analysis of controlled dynamical systems is an important tool for safety-critical control under uncertainty, in which the reach-avoid probability is characterized by a Hamilton-Jacobi partial differential equation (PDE). However, solving this PDE using conventional numerical methods becomes computationally intractable as the system dimension increases. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) may converge to inaccurate local minima when trained primarily through PDE-residual minimization. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a scalable alternative, but its learned value functions may be inaccurate or inconsistent with the governing PDE. This paper proposes a physics-informed RL (PIRL) framework that combines the complementary strengths of PINNs and RL for stochastic reach-avoid analysis. We develop a scheduled PIRL algorithm in which temporal-difference actor-critic learning first guides the critic toward a meaningful approximation of the reach-avoid value function. PDE-residual and boundary-condition losses are then introduced progressively to enforce consistency with the governing PDE and its boundary conditions. The proposed method mitigates the failure modes of conventional PINN techniques while achieving accuracy comparable to that of successfully trained PINNs. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is demonstrated through two case studies.

</details>


### [122] [A (Purely) Graph-Theoretic Approach to Synchronization of Nonlinear Dynamical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17755)
*Aandrew Baggio Sahaya Arokiadoss,G. Arunkumar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种纯图论框架，利用单个Lipschitz类界限直接从有向图计算耦合强度，以实现非线性动力学网络的同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理大规模网络时因需解矩阵不等式或依赖详细系统模型而失效，且对连通性模式敏感导致不可行性问题。

Method: 采用仅基于有向图结构和单个Lipschitz类动态约束的方法，通过分析每个强连通分量中的n-1条有向路径来计算耦合强度，避免求解矩阵不等式。

Result: 该方法将路径分析数量从n(n-1)/2减少到n-1，复杂度降至O(n^3)，在多种连通模式下均能成功同步大型非线性动力学网络。

Conclusion: 网络连通性可用于同步一大类非线性动力学网络，所提方法简化了计算并克服了传统方法在可行性上的局限。

Abstract: Synchronizing nonlinear dynamical networks typically requires solving matrix inequalities or detailed system models, which fail for large networks. This paper offers a simple fix : a purely graph-theoretic framework using only a single Lipschitz-like bound on the dynamics. Coupling strengths are computed directly from the digraph, bypassing inequality solvers entirely. The method succeeds where existing approaches encounter infeasibility due to connectivity patterns. It examines only $n-1$ directed paths per strongly connected component versus $\frac{n(n-1)}{2}$ undirected paths before, achieving $O(n^3)$ complexity. Results show network connectivity can be exploited to synchronize a large class of nonlinear dynamical networks.

</details>


### [123] [A Hybrid Discrete-Event and Agent-Based Simulation Approach to Model Circular Supply Chains in Healthcare: A Case Study of Laparoscopic Scissors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17142)
*Mohd Shoaib,Antuela Tako,Shahin Rahimifard*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本研究通过模拟方法评估了在医疗供应链中引入循环产品的效果，以腹腔镜剪刀供应链为案例，首次从个体成员和整体系统层面分析了循环产品设计的影响，并探讨了支持循环经济转型的工具开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预测向循环经济转型结果的方法有限且多为静态，难以应对医疗供应链中的复杂性和不确定性。

Method: 采用仿真模型评估引入循环产品对医疗供应链各环节的影响，考虑流程和性能中的变异性，以腹腔镜剪刀供应链为案例进行分析。

Result: 引入循环产品可减少环境影响，但要实现成本和排放的显著降低需要大量前期投资；该模型还可用于优化医院库存策略，保障患者安全和运营稳定。

Conclusion: 仿真方法有助于理解循环医疗供应链的动态影响，支持决策制定，推动循环经济在医疗领域的实践发展。

Abstract: Circular healthcare supply chains are inherently complex, characterised by interdependencies among their actors and high uncertainty in product flows and performance. Current methods used to predict the outcomes of transitioning to circular economy (CE) are limited and mostly static. This paper demonstrates the use of simulation to assess the effect of introducing circular products and the implications across the healthcare supply chain accounting for variability. The laparoscopic scissors supply chain is chosen as a case study example. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study that assesses the implications of introducing circular product (medical devices) designs at both the individual supply chain member and overall system level. The model can be also used to inform optimal inventory strategies for hospitals, to ensure that patient safety and hospital operations are maintained. Our findings suggest that adopting circular products can reduce the environmental impact, but to achieve significant reductions in both cost and emissions, it requires significant upfront investment. We discuss the theoretical and practical implications of our study in developing tools to support the transition to CE.

</details>


### [124] [Safe Deep Reinforcement Learning for Energy-Efficient HVAC Control in Multi-Zone Residential Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17235)
*Oussama Ziadi,Abdelilah Rochd,Samir Idrissi Kaitouni,Mohamed Oualid Mghazli,Adnane Saoud*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种安全认证的深度强化学习框架用于多区域住宅HVAC控制，结合PPO和SAC算法在EnergyPlus/Sinergym仿真中优化能耗与热舒适性，并通过Lipschitz-based前向不变性分析对PPO策略进行训练后安全认证，确保约束满足。实验结果表明该方法在八区域VRF测试平台具有显著节能和舒适性提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HVAC控制策略难以协调多区域间的能耗与舒适性权衡，且强化学习在安全关键系统中的应用受限于缺乏形式化安全保证。

Method: 采用PPO和SAC两种深度强化学习算法，在EnergyPlus/Sinergym仿真环境中训练多区域HVAC控制器；使用基于Lipschitz常数的前向不变性分析对训练后的PPO策略进行安全认证，以提供形式化安全保证。

Result: 在八区域VRF测试平台上，PPO代理相比规则控制减少了67%的舒适性违规，SAC代理实现了27.6%的节能；PPO策略通过了形式化安全认证，安全裕度为2.003°C。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与训练后安全验证相结合，可在保证安全性的同时有效优化多区域建筑HVAC系统的能耗与热舒适性，验证了该方法在实际建筑控制中的可行性。

Abstract: HVAC systems represent a major share of building energy consumption. Traditional control strategies are limited in coordinating energy-comfort tradeoffs across multiple zones simultaneously. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers adaptive, data-driven control that optimizes performance over time. However, deploying learned neural network controllers in safety-critical building systems remains challenging due to lack of formal safety guarantees. We propose a safety-certified deep RL framework for multi-zone residential HVAC control. Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agents are trained in an EnergyPlus/Sinergym simulation to minimize energy consumption while maintaining thermal comfort. Post-training safety certification is performed on the PPO policy using Lipschitz-based forward invariance analysis, building on existing tools for the computation of Lipschitz constants for neural networks, to guarantee constraint satisfaction. Both agents are evaluated over an annual simulation cycle in an eight-zone variable refrigerant flow (VRF) testbed. The PPO agent achieves 67\% comfort violation reduction compared to rule-based control, while the SAC agent achieves 27.6\% energy savings. The PPO policy satisfies formal safety certification with a margin of $2.003^\circ$C. These results demonstrate the feasibility of combining reinforcement learning with post-training safety verification for multi-zone building control.

</details>


### [125] [Assessing Collision Probability in Low-Thrust Deorbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17245)
*Shuta Fukii,Daisuke Sakai,Yasuhiro Yoshimura,Yuri Matsushita,Toshiya Hanada,Yuki Itaya,Tadanori Fukushima*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文评估了低推力脱轨轨迹中的碰撞风险，并研究了再入时间与碰撞风险之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高卫星寿命结束后的处理合规率，维护太空环境，需要对低推力脱轨任务中的碰撞风险进行评估。

Method: 通过参数化研究分析低推力（如激光）引起的低水平减速对目标轨道高度的影响，以及脱轨过程中碰撞风险的变化。

Result: 揭示了再入时间与碰撞风险之间的关系，发现低推力轨迹耗时较长，显著增加了碰撞风险。

Conclusion: 低推力脱轨任务中必须考虑碰撞风险，优化再入时间有助于提升任务成功率和空间环境安全性。

Abstract: End-of-life support of satellites is necessary to improve post-mission-disposal compliance rates for maintaining space environment. Deorbit mission with low thrust, e.g. a laser, induces a low-level deceleration on the target object that gradually lowers the target altitude. Since such a low-thrust trajectory is time-consuming, the risk of collision greatly influences the mission success rate. In this context, this paper assesses the collision risk during deorbit trajectories with low thrust. Furthermore, parametric studies for the relationship between the re-entry time and the risk of collision are performed.

</details>


### [126] [Balancing a Flying Inverted Pendulum with an Unknown Length Using Model Predictive Control and a Genetic Algorithm Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17256)
*Esther Paul,Mitchell Torok,Mohammad Deghat*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于在线遗传算法估计器和模型预测控制的方法，用于解决摆长未知的飞行倒立摆实际实验问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决飞行倒立摆系统中参数（如摆长）未知以及外部扰动影响下的控制难题。

Method: 采用在线遗传算法（GA）进行参数估计，并结合模型预测控制（MPC）实现对飞行倒立摆的精确控制。

Result: 在实际系统上验证了该方法的有效性，MPC控制器表现出良好的鲁棒性和轨迹跟踪精度，GA估计器在不同初始条件下均实现了快速收敛和高估计精度。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效应对参数不确定性和外部干扰，适用于复杂动态系统的实时控制。

Abstract: This paper proposes an online Genetic Algorithm (GA) estimator and a Model Predictive Control (MPC) approach to solve the flying inverted pendulum problem in a practical experiment where the pendulum length is unknown. The performance of the MPC approach was demonstrated on a practical system through disturbance and trajectory tracking tests to assess controller robustness and tracking accuracy. The convergence speed and accuracy of the online GA estimator were validated on a practical system using different initial conditions.

</details>


### [127] [A Hybrid End-to-End and Modular Control Architecture Toward Safe Vehicle Lateral Control: Combining Soft Actor-Critic with Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17258)
*Farzaneh Tatari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合软演员-评论家（SAC）策略与约束线性模型预测控制（MPC）的混合架构，用于自动驾驶车辆的横向控制，兼顾学习策略的适应性和MPC的安全性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有横向控制器在准确性、安全性与适应性之间的权衡问题，尤其是模型误差和传感器噪声下的性能下降，以及纯学习方法缺乏安全保证的问题。

Method: 设计一种混合控制架构，将SAC策略与线性MPC结合，使用MPC的第一步最优解作为模型基础锚点，并通过一个单调融合系数在两者之间插值，实现端到端学习与模型驱动控制的融合。

Result: 在多种实验条件下，该混合架构保持了独立SAC的跟踪精度，同时始终满足MPC的执行器约束，并保留了基于模型的确定性控制贡献；但无法完全防止分布偏移下的边界情况失效。

Conclusion: 该架构通过构造提供了执行器范围保障，但在递归可行性与终端不变性方面仍有局限；未来可通过V2X信号调度融合系数或引入约束二次规划预测安全滤波器来改进。

Abstract: Connected and automated vehicles demand lateral controllers that are simultaneously accurate, low-effort, and safe under model error and sensor noise. Modular controllers such as model predictive control (MPC) are interpretable and constraint-aware but rely on accurate models and hand-tuned weights. End-to-end learned policies, in particular continuous-action deep reinforcement learning, are adaptable and require no hand-designed control law, but offer no intrinsic safety guarantees and limited interpretability. This paper presents a hybrid architecture that combines an end-to-end Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) policy with a constrained linear MPC into a single steering command, using the MPC's first-step optimum as the model-based anchor and a single monotone blending coefficient that interpolates between the two paradigms. The architecture is evaluated on a linearized lateral bicycle model against a PID baseline, a tuned linear MPC, and a stand-alone SAC policy, across nominal, single-axis robustness, and multi-initial-condition ensemble experiments. The hybrid retains the tracking quality of stand-alone SAC while remaining inside the MPC's actuator envelope and preserving a deterministic, model-based contribution to every steering command. The architecture provides an actuator-envelope guarantee by construction but does not establish recursive feasibility or terminal invariance, and the closed-form blend does not prevent all corner-case divergences at the boundary of the training distribution. A corner-case analysis shows that the blend attenuates but cannot prevent failure under distribution shift, motivating a connectivity-aware extension in which the blending coefficient is scheduled by vehicle-to-everything (V2X) signals to restore model-based authority. Limitations and a path toward a constrained-QP predictive safety filter are discussed.

</details>


### [128] [Safe whole-body backstepping control for quadcopter path-following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17259)
*Arthur H. D. Nunes,Arthur Da C. Vangasse,Guilherme V. Raffo,Vinicius M. Gonçalves,Luciano C. A. Pimenta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Backstepping和高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）的四旋翼全机身路径跟踪与避障控制方法，结合人工矢量场实现引导与姿态控制，并通过仿真和实际实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现四旋翼在复杂环境下的安全路径跟踪，需同时保证路径收敛性和实时避障能力，传统方法难以兼顾稳定性与安全性。

Method: 采用基于四元数的刚体动力学建模，结合Backstepping控制设计整体控制律；利用人工矢量场（AVF）构建集成导引控制（IGC）以确保路径收敛，并引入HOCBF框架下的平滑距离函数增强反应式避障能力。

Result: 所提方法在有无障碍情况下均能实现渐近路径收敛，并有效避免碰撞；通过SIL仿真和Crazyflie真实平台实验验证了控制策略的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结合Backstepping与HOCBF的控制架构能够有效实现四旋翼的安全路径跟踪，为小型无人机在受限环境中的自主飞行提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel whole-body Backstepping control strategy for safe quadcopter path-following. The proposed approach introduces an integrated control scheme that combines a translational guidance controller with a rigid-body attitude controller. To guarantee asymptotic path convergence, the method utilizes a nominal Integrated Guidance and Control (IGC) based on Artificial Vector Fields (AVF). To ensure reactive safety and collision avoidance, the control law is modified using a smooth distance function within the High-Order Control Barrier Function (HOCBF) framework. The quadcopter dynamics are modeled using quaternion algebra to represent position, velocity, and attitude. By combining the Backstepping approach with HOCBF, the controller guarantees that the vehicle avoids obstacle sets while successfully converging to the target path when unobstructed. The proposed methodology is validated through software-in-the-loop simulations and real-world experimental results using the Crazyflie platform.

</details>


### [129] [Nonadaptive Learning in Robust Nonlinear Output Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17262)
*Shimin Wang,Martin Guay,Richard D. Braatz*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高相对阶非线性输出反馈系统的鲁棒非自适应调节方法，结合输入驱动滤波器、内模原理与递归反步法，将调节问题转化为增广误差系统的鲁棒输入-状态稳定问题，实现了全局渐近调节，并通过Duffing系统验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有任意高相对阶的非线性输出反馈系统，现有自适应调节方法存在依赖参数化回归模型和复杂Lyapunov函数构造的问题，且在系统动态复杂或部分未知时性能受限，因此需要一种不依赖参数估计的鲁棒非自适应控制策略。

Method: 提出一种非自适应设计方法，结合输入驱动滤波器与通用内模，并采用递归反步法设计控制器；通过构建增广误差系统，将输出调节问题转化为鲁棒输入-状态稳定问题，在不依赖线性参数化回归模型和仅需标准外系统假设下实现控制目标。

Result: 在标准外系统假设（纯虚数且单重特征值）和内部动态最小相位输入-状态稳定条件下，实现了全局渐近调节；给出了可显式计算并验证的设计增益选择不等式，确保即使在系统动态复杂或部分已知的情况下，估计误差和跟踪误差仍能收敛。

Conclusion: 所提出的非自适应框架有效解决了高相对阶非线性系统的鲁棒输出调节问题，避免了传统自适应方法中的参数估计和复杂Lyapunov函数构造，具有更强的鲁棒性和更广的适用性，理论结果在Duffing系统上得到验证。

Abstract: This paper considers robust nonadaptive regulation for general nonlinear systems in an output-feedback setting with arbitrarily high relative degree. We develop a nonadaptive design that combines an input-driven filter and a generic internal model with a recursive backstepping law, thereby recasting the regulation problem as the robust input-to-state stabilization of an augmented error system. Unlike adaptive schemes, the proposed method does not rely on linearly parameterized regressors and does not require the construction of Lyapunov functions having merely nonpositive derivatives. Under standard assumptions on the exosystem, including purely imaginary and simple eigenvalues, together with a minimum-phase input-to-state stability condition on the internal dynamics, we establish global asymptotic regulation and derive explicit, verifiable inequalities for selecting the design gains. The resulting nonadaptive framework guarantees convergence of the estimation and tracking errors even when the controlled-system dynamics are complex or only partially known. The effectiveness of the theoretical results is demonstrated using a benchmark controlled Duffing system.

</details>


### [130] [Integrated Heat and Power System Scheduling with Continuous-Time Thermal Dynamics via Bernstein-Galerkin Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17287)
*Jie Deng,Zhigang Li,J. H. Zheng,Ye Guo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑区域供热网络连续时间热动态特性的电-热综合系统调度框架，采用Bernstein-Galerkin变换方法将偏微分方程约束转化为代数约束，提升了模型精度与调度经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区域供热网络调度模型多采用简化的离散时间模型，难以准确描述连续的时空热动态特性，导致灵活性评估偏差和调度方案次优。

Method: 提出一种集成热力与电力系统的调度框架，引入Bernstein-Galerkin变换方法，将描述热动态的偏微分方程转化为有限维代数约束，从而在保持动态保真度的同时实现可解优化。

Result: 所提方法相比传统离散化方法能更准确地刻画热动态过程，仿真结果表明其在经济性和系统可靠性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了连续热动态建模难题，为电-热协同调度提供了更高精度、更可靠的优化模型。

Abstract: Coordinated scheduling of district heating networks (DHNs) and electric power systems can improve operational flexibility and reduce costs by exploiting thermal inertia. Most existing formulations rely on simplified discrete-time DHN models, which may inadequately represent continuous spatiotemporal thermal dynamics and can lead to biased flexibility estimation and suboptimal schedules. In this paper, an integrated heat and power system scheduling framework that explicitly incorporates the continuous-time thermal dynamics of DHNs is proposed. A Bernstein-Galerkin transform method is developed to convert the underlying partial-differential thermal-dynamics constraints into a finite set of algebraic constraints, enabling tractable optimization while retaining dynamic fidelity. The resulting model transforms the original infinite-dimensional variational problem into a finite-dimensional coefficient optimization that can be solved using optimization solvers. Compared with conventional discretization approaches, the proposed method provides a more accurate representation of thermal dynamics and yields schedules with improved economic performance and reliability.

</details>


### [131] [Universal Approximation of Maximal Lyapunov Functions with Anchored Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17290)
*Jun Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于神经网络的Lyapunov函数构造方法，能够在同伦稳定向量场主导的系统中实现半全局逼近，并通过数值例子验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服在神经近似中保持最大Lyapunov函数严格递减的困难，尤其是在平衡点处边界消失的问题。

Method: 构建了一个连续可微的最大目标函数和一个锚定的、保正的神经网络函数族，利用嵌套不变子水平集实现Lyapunov函数及其一阶导数的半全局通用逼近。

Result: 证明了严格神经Lyapunov函数可以在耗尽吸引域的嵌套不变子水平集上逼近目标函数，并提供了可直接验证的认证条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经网络架构能够有效构造并验证最大Lyapunov函数，适用于局部由渐近稳定齐次向量场主导的动力系统。

Abstract: Maximal Lyapunov functions encode the entire domain of attraction of an asymptotically stable equilibrium, but preserving strict decrease under neural approximation is difficult because its margin vanishes at the equilibrium. For systems locally dominated by an asymptotically stable homogeneous vector field, we construct a continuously differentiable maximal target and an anchored, positivity-preserving neural family. We prove semiglobal universal approximation: strict neural Lyapunov functions and their first derivatives can approximate the target on nested invariant sublevel sets that exhaust the domain of attraction. We also provide directly verifiable conditions under which a candidate neural Lyapunov function can be formally certified, and illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed neural architecture through numerical examples.

</details>


### [132] [Infinite-Horizon Sparse Optimal Control: Solution through a Finite-Horizon Subproblem and Its Receding-Horizon Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17464)
*Yasuaki Oishi,Takumi Iwata,Masaaki Nagahara*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限时域稀疏最优控制问题，提出可通过有限时域子问题求解，并给出了子问题时域长度的估计及自适应选择方法，同时讨论了滚动时域实现及其最优性与稀疏性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于稀疏控制在有限时域内已有较多研究，而无限时域下的稀疏控制缺乏有效求解方法，本文旨在建立无限时域稀疏控制与有限时域优化之间的联系。

Method: 利用最优解在尾部具有稀疏性（即控制输入最终恒为零）的特点，将无限时域问题转化为有限时域子问题，并结合自适应和滚动时域策略进行求解。

Result: 证明了无限时域稀疏控制的最优解可通过有限时域子问题获得，给出了所需时域长度的估计，并实现了具有最优性和稀疏性的滚动时域控制。

Conclusion: 无限时域稀疏最优控制问题可通过有限时域方法有效求解，且其稀疏特性可被充分利用以简化计算和实现。

Abstract: Sparse optimal control is considered in the infinite horizon. In the literature, sparse control has been considered mostly in a finite horizon for its formulation into a finite-dimensional optimization problem. It is shown in this paper that an optimal solution of the infinite-horizon sparse control problem can be obtained through a solution of some finite-horizon subproblem. This is due to sparsity of the optimal solution in the sense that the optimal control input is constantly equal to zero at its tail. An estimate is given on the horizon length required by this subproblem and its adaptive choice is also discussed. Implementation with a receding-horizon technique is considered and its optimality and sparsity are guaranteed.

</details>


### [133] [Communication Reduction via Semantic-Based Encoding in DMPC Using LSTMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17592)
*Torben Schiz,Pedro H. J. Nardelli,Henrik Ebel*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LSTM编码器-解码器网络的语义通信方法，用于降低分布式模型预测控制（DMPC）中的通信负担。代理发送压缩消息并由接收方重建，显著减少了通信需求，同时保持了良好的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式模型预测控制（DMPC）中每步需大量信息交换，导致通信压力巨大，限制了其在无线系统中的应用。

Method: 采用基于LSTM的编码器-解码器网络，将原始消息压缩为低维表示进行传输，接收端重构原始消息，并集成到分布式优化算法中。

Result: 在移动机器人编队实验中，该方法在减少通信量的同时保持了满意的控制性能；LSTM实现了高精度的消息重建，并支持不同预测时域长度而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义通信方法有效降低了DMPC的通信开销，提升了系统在资源受限环境下的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: The communication demands of distributed model prediction control (DMPC) can overwhelm even advanced wireless communication technologies as agents must exchange a significant amount of information at least once per time step. To semantically reduce communication demands, this work employs encoder-decoder networks built around long-short term memory (LSTM) cells in a distributed optimization algorithm. Agents publish a reduced representation of a message and receivers reconstruct the original message upon reception. In tests with reduced communication using formations of mobile robots, trained networks retain satisfactory performance and work reliably under conditions overwhelming full communication. As the results show, the usage of LSTMs either allows unprecedented reconstruction accuracy or the usage of different prediction-horizon lengths without the necessity to retrain.

</details>


### [134] [On the behavior assignment problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17652)
*Francesca Mazzolani,Michelangelo Bin,Lorenzo Marconi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了非线性系统的渐近行为分配问题，旨在设计控制器使得闭环系统在任意输入下复现参考系统的输入输出行为，涵盖经典模型匹配、干扰抑制等问题，并通过引入同步检测信号将问题转化为标准镇定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的控制方法通常依赖于显式的跟踪误差或调节误差进行反馈设计，但在许多实际系统中这些误差不可直接获取。因此需要一种新的框架来处理闭环系统动态非自治情况下的稳态行为设计问题。

Method: 在SISO标准型设定下，提出一个同步检测信号，其收敛至零等价于成功实现行为分配，从而将原问题转化为标准的稳定性控制问题。

Result: 给出了行为分配问题可解的充分条件，并通过隧道二极管电路和单摆频率匹配两个例子验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够有效解决无显式误差反馈的非线性系统渐近行为设计问题，避免人为指定特定稳态，具有更广的适用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces the asymptotic behavior assignment problem for nonlinear systems. Given a controlled system and a reference system with an ``open'' input, the goal is to design a regulator such that, for every admissible input, the asymptotic input-output behavior of the closed-loop system reproduces that of the reference. This formulation captures, as special cases, classical model matching, disturbance rejection, and master-slave synchronization, but does not assume that an explicit tracking or regulation error is available for feedback. Motivated by nonlinear output regulation, we discuss how steady-state concepts for autonomous systems must be adapted when the closed-loop dynamics is not autonomous. In a SISO normal-form setting we devise sufficient conditions for the solution of the behavior assignment problem by introducing a synchrony-detection signal whose convergence to zero is equivalent to successful behavior assignment, thereby reducing the problem to a standard stabilization one. Two examples, a tunnel-diode circuit with multiple input-dependent equilibria, and a pendulum frequency-matching problem, illustrate how the proposed framework avoids artificially selecting a specific steady state.

</details>


### [135] [Fault detection on manifolds of nonlinear dynamical systems with dual autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17698)
*Bulut Kuşkonmaz,Szymon Greś,Rafał Wi{ś}niewski*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Koopman算子理论和两阶段自编码器的可解释性自编码器方法，用于非线性随机机械系统中的参数故障检测，并通过假设检验框架实现故障检测，在仿真和实际基准测试中表现优于标准自编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自编码器在故障检测中缺乏对非线性动力学的可解释性，限制了其在故障定位与量化等高级诊断中的应用。

Method: 利用Koopman算子理论构建输出数据的数学表示，将数据嵌入流形并用两阶段自编码器进行逼近；在假设检验框架下，通过新数据与名义流形邻域的一致性进行故障检测。

Result: 在包含两种非线性的玩具机械系统上进行了蒙特卡洛仿真，并应用于两个知名真实基准，故障检测性能优于标准自编码器。

Conclusion: 该方法提升了自编码器在非线性系统中故障检测的可解释性和诊断能力，具有良好的检测性能和应用潜力。

Abstract: Autoencoders are commonly used for unsupervised data-driven fault detection in nonlinear dynamical systems. Despite their widespread success and often favorable performance compared with traditional approaches, most applications rely on heuristic reconstruction of measured data using features learned from nominal training data, without explicit insight into the underlying nonlinear dynamics. This lack of interpretability limits the extension of autoencoder-based fault detection methods to higher levels of fault diagnosis, e.g., fault localization and quantification, and confines their use largely to application-oriented studies. To address this limitation, we propose a strategy for detecting parametric faults in nonlinear stochastic mechanical systems. A mathematical representation of the output data is developed using Koopman operator theory, which motivates their embedding on a manifold and its subsequent approximation with a two-stage autoencoder. Fault detection is formulated within a hypothesis-testing framework, in which new data are tested for consistency with a neighborhood of the manifold identified from nominal observations. The proposed method is validated through Monte Carlo simulations of a toy mechanical system with two types of nonlinearity and applied to two well-known real benchmarks, where it provides favorable fault-detection performance compared with standard autoencoders.

</details>


### [136] [Edge-Native Embodied Intelligence for Action-Aware Wireless Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17774)
*Yiru Wang,Chuanao Jiang,Jiahui Cui,Zide Fan,Lei Wang,Zehui Xiong,Dong In Kim*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向6G的边缘原生具身智能（ENEI）框架，通过双向的边缘-具身循环，实现具身代理与边缘认知服务的协同进化，兼顾开放认知与资源约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能系统在开放世界认知与资源受限部署之间存在矛盾：本地模型能力有限，云端方案延迟高且不可靠，而现有边缘智能缺乏物理交互意识和主动经验获取机制。

Method: 提出ENEI框架，构建基于6G通信的双向环路：从边缘到具身，通过置信度感知辅助和生成式自适应增强本地自主性；从具身到边缘，通过体验价值引导的主动具身联邦学习，利用物理行为生成有价值的经验用于模型更新。6G网络支持以目标为导向的传输和可编程资源分配。

Result: 通过两个案例研究验证了该框架在异常分布下的无人机导航和移动驱动联邦学习中的可行性和通信效率。

Conclusion: ENEI实现了边缘认知与具身行动的相互增强，为可扩展、自适应、自进化的具身无线系统提供了统一架构。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is shifting artificial intelligence from passive digital perception toward active physical interaction. However, foundation-model-enabled embodied agents face a fundamental tension between open-world cognition and resource-constrained deployment. On-device models are limited by computation, memory, and energy budgets, whereas cloud-centric solutions introduce latency and reliability risks over dynamic wireless links. Edge general intelligence provides a promising cognitive backbone, but existing frameworks still lack physical grounding, action awareness, and mechanisms for actively acquiring useful physical experience. To address these limitations, this article introduces edge-native embodied intelligence (ENEI), an action-aware wireless edge framework that integrates embodied agents, the 6G communication and networking fabric, and edge cognitive services into a 6G-mediated bidirectional edge-embodiment loop. Along the edge-to-embodiment axis, confidence-aware assistance and edge-driven generative adaptation enhance local autonomy under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions. Along the embodiment-to-edge axis, value-of-experience guided active embodied federated learning enables physical actions to generate informative experience for continuous edge model evolution. The 6G fabric supports both directions through goal-oriented transmission and programmable radio-resource allocation. Two case studies on OOD drone navigation and mobility-driven federated learning illustrate the feasibility and communication efficiency of the proposed mechanisms. ENEI provides a unified perspective in which edge cognition strengthens embodied action, while embodied agency actively enriches edge cognition, laying the foundation for scalable, adaptive, and self-evolving embodied wireless systems.

</details>


### [137] [The Zonotopic Mixture Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17897)
*Rodrigo A. González,Angel L. Cedeño,Vicenç Puig*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于zonotope混合噪声模型的状态估计方法，通过结合概率与有界不确定性框架，实现了具有统计保证的可靠状态包络估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统状态估计方法在概率框架或未知但有界框架中分别处理噪声，但二者难以兼顾统计信息与确定性边界；本文旨在弥合两种噪声描述之间的差距。

Method: 提出zonotope混合噪声模型，采用多个zonotope按固定概率生成噪声，并设计zonotope混合滤波器，通过维护模式历史的zonotopic卡尔曼滤波器组、剔除被数据否定的历史并加权保留项，结合贪婪混合缩减策略以保持计算可行性。

Result: 所提方法生成的状态包络既提供保证的覆盖概率，又对所有符合边界的噪声实现保持有效性，数值例子验证了其相对于相关方法的潜在优势。

Conclusion: zonotope混合滤波器成功融合了概率与有界不确定性建模的优点，在保持计算可扩展的同时提供了兼具统计意义和确定性保证的状态估计结果。

Abstract: State estimation is commonly posed in either a probabilistic or an unknown-but-bounded framework. The former requires a fully specified noise distribution, typically with unbounded support, while the latter yields guaranteed enclosures that carry no probabilistic weighting. Bridging these noise descriptions, this paper proposes a zonotopic mixture noise model, in which the noise is generated by drawing a zonotope from a finite collection according to fixed probabilities and then realizing an arbitrary element of it. For this noise model, we derive the zonotopic mixture filter, which propagates a bank of zonotopic Kalman filters over mode histories, discards the histories falsified by the data, and weights the surviving ones by their relative probability. The resulting state enclosures yield guaranteed coverage probabilities and remain valid for every noise realization compatible with the bounds, and a greedy mixture reduction scheme preserves these statistical guarantees while keeping the representation tractable. Numerical examples illustrate the proposed approach and its potential benefits over related state estimation methods.

</details>


### [138] [Adaptive Model Predictive Control for Ground Vehicles: Review and Demonstrative Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17902)
*Chetana Gadgil,Mahendra Singh Tomar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了用于自动驾驶车辆的自适应模型预测控制（AMPC）方法，涵盖增益调度、在线模型估计、权重与视界自适应、基于学习及混合MPC等技术，并通过仿真展示了其在轨迹跟踪中的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型预测控制在面对模型误差、计算复杂性和动态环境时存在局限，需通过自适应机制提升自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性与实时性。

Method: 对现有AMPC方法进行分类综述，包括增益调度MPC、在线模型估计MPC、权重自适应MPC、视界自适应MPC、基于学习的MPC及混合MPC，并结合仿真验证控制器在权重与速度自适应方面的性能。

Result: 系统梳理了各类AMPC方法的特点与适用场景，仿真结果表明自适应机制能有效提升轨迹跟踪精度与控制灵活性。

Conclusion: AMPC通过实时调整模型、代价函数或约束，显著增强了对不确定性和动态环境的适应能力，是提升自动驾驶控制性能的关键方向。

Abstract: This paper reviews Adaptive Model Predictive Control (AMPC) methods for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), focusing on control strategies that dynamically adapt to uncertainties and changing conditions in real-time. The critical role of Adaptive Model Predictive Control (AMPC) in addressing the challenges of autonomous vehicle control are discussed. For the scope of this paper, AMPC is defined as a class of Model Predictive Control (MPC) techniques that modify the system model, cost function, constraints, or prediction horizon, based on real-time data. Traditional MPC, while effective for constrained optimization, struggles with model inaccuracies, computational demands, and dynamic environments, necessitating AMPC methods. The review covers existing literature on Gain scheduled MPC, Online Model Estimation MPC, Weight Adaptive MPC, Horizon Adaptive MPC, Learning Based MPC, and Hybrid MPC that combines MPC with other control methods. In addition to the survey, a demonstrative simulation of an adaptive MPC controller is presented that illustrates practical aspects of weight and speed adaptation in trajectory tracking.

</details>


### [139] [Steady-State Equivalent Circuit Model for Data Center Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17925)
*Muhammad Hamza Ali,Peng Sang,Hyeon Woo,Hyein Kang,Sungyun Choi,Amritanshu Pandey*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于数据中心的稳态等效电路模型（ECM），该模型显式地建模了IT负载、电源单元、冷却和辅助系统，能够反映异构工作负载分布对电网侧需求的影响，并揭示传统聚合模型忽略的效率变化和线路过载风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心在电力系统稳态研究中通常被简化为恒定PQ或ZIP聚合负载模型，这类模型忽略了IT设备内部的电力电子转换过程、服务器利用率与电网需求之间的电气关系，且假设工作负载在集群中均匀分布，导致无法准确反映实际运行中的损耗和效率变化。因此，需要一种更精确的模型来捕捉数据中心内部动态对电网的影响。

Method: 提出一种稳态等效电路模型（ECM），分别对IT负载、电源单元（PSU）、冷却系统和辅助系统建立电路模型；其中，PSU模型显式表示其内部电力电子转换阶段，IT负载则采用基于服务器利用率的功耗模型，并与考虑损耗的PSU-ECM结合；该模型保持与传统潮流分析的兼容性，并通过蒙特卡洛仿真在大规模输电系统潮流中进行验证，比较异构与同构集群利用率下的线路负载情况。

Result: 相比固定效率的恒定PQ模型，所提出的ECM模型显示，在约30%的蒙特卡洛样本中，最紧张的线路会超过其热极限；此外，结果表明，假设服务器利用率均匀分布会高估线路负载的变异性，相较于异构利用情况，变异性被高估了17%至46%，具体取决于集群内工作负载的相关性。

Conclusion: 所提出的等效电路模型能更真实地反映数据中心在电网中的电气行为，揭示了传统聚合模型在评估线路安全性和负载波动方面的不足，强调了考虑服务器利用率分布和电源转换效率的重要性，适用于未来高精度的电网-数据中心协同分析。

Abstract: Planners currently represent data centers as aggregate constant-PQ or ZIP loads in steady-state interconnection and contingency studies. These aggregate models are computationally convenient. However, they obscure the electrical relationship between computational workloads, server utilization, and grid-side demand. They ignore the internal power-electronic conversion stages of IT loads and assume homogeneous workload distributions across the compute clusters. This hides operating-point-dependent converter losses and efficiency variations. We propose a steady-state equivalent-circuit model (ECM) for data centers, which explicitly builds circuit models for IT loads, power supply units, cooling, and auxiliary systems. For power supply units, the equivalent circuit model explicitly represents internal power-electronic conversion stages. For IT loads, we develop a utilization-dependent server power model, and we combine it with loss-aware ECMs of power supply units. This approach captures the grid-side impact of heterogeneous workload distributions while preserving compatibility with conventional power-flow analysis. We evaluate this data center ECM in large-scale transmission power flows, using Monte Carlo simulations under heterogeneous and homogeneous cluster utilization. In comparison with the fixed-efficiency constant-PQ model, the ECM predicts that the most stressed line exceeds its thermal limit in about 30% of Monte Carlo samples. The results further show that homogeneous server utilization overstates line-loading variability by 17%-46% relative to heterogeneous server utilization, depending on the intra-cluster workload correlation.

</details>


### [140] [The geometric Laplace transform: Definition, existence and properties of the Geometric Algebra Laplace transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18043)
*Manel Velasco,Arnau Dòria-Cerezo,Isiah Zaplana*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了在几何代数（GA）框架下的拉普拉斯变换定义，适用于符号小于或等于5的几何代数，扩展了之前在超复数代数上的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在动态系统（特别是电路）建模与控制中应用几何代数，需要将系统的微分方程从实域转换到拉普拉斯域，因此需在几何代数中定义拉普拉斯变换。

Method: 基于已有在超复数代数中的拉普拉斯变换工作，扩展并提出适用于特定几何代数结构的拉普拉斯变换定义，并分析其性质。

Result: 成功建立了适用于签名不超过5的几何代数的拉普拉斯变换定义及其基本性质。

Conclusion: 该定义为在几何代数框架下分析和控制动态系统提供了数学基础，推动了几何代数在工程领域的应用。

Abstract: Recent publications have started to explore the application of Geometric Algebra (GA) to the modeling, analysis and control of dynamical systems and, in particular, electrical circuits. Since a crucial element there is to transform the ordinary differential equations governing the dynamical system which models the systems' behavior from the real domain to the Laplace domain, a definition of the Laplace transform in GA is needed. In the present work, we extend previous works dealing with extension to some hiper-complex algebras by introducing a definition of the Laplace transform within the framework of Geometric Algebra (GA). In particular, our definition and its properties are applicable to geometric algebras with signature lower or equal than 5.

</details>


### [141] [A Coalitional Game for Demand-Side Management in a Micro-Grid with Multiple Electricity Retailers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17934)
*Pablo R Baldivieso-Monasterios,Fernando Genis Mendoza,George Konstantopoulos,Dario Bauso*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多零售商电力网络需求侧管理框架，通过联盟博弈和多目标优化算法联合优化电价、用电需求和网络分区，实现了零售商利润与用户福利的平衡，并扩展至风险感知定价，仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多零售商电力市场中，如何协调竞争与合作、平衡零售商利润与消费者福利，并考虑风险因素，是需求侧管理中的关键挑战。本文旨在建立一个能够处理这些复杂交互的统一框架。

Method: 将零售商间的互动建模为联盟博弈，形成一组耦合的混合整数优化问题；提出基于多目标优化的联盟形成算法，并证明其在有限步内收敛并获得弱帕累托有效解的一个子集；进一步引入条件风险价值（CVaR）构建风险共享模型。

Result: 算法能在有限步内收敛，找到兼顾零售商利润与消费者福利的弱帕累托有效解；数值仿真显示该方法能生成多种可接受的权衡方案，形成稳定的均衡分区集合；框架支持对竞争、联盟形成和风险感知定价行为进行分析。

Conclusion: 所提框架为多零售商需求侧管理系统中的竞争与合作提供了可计算的分析工具，能够在保证收敛性的前提下实现多方利益的协调，并支持风险意识决策，具有良好的理论性质与应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper develops a demand-side management framework for electricity networks with multiple competing retailers. The interaction among retailers is formulated as a coalitional game, yielding a family of coupled mixed-integer optimisation problems in which retail prices, consumer power demands, and the network partition are jointly optimised. To solve this problem, we propose a coalition-formation algorithm based on multi-objective optimisation principles. The algorithm seeks to identify coalition structures that balance retailer profit and consumer welfare. We prove that the proposed algorithm converges in a finite number of steps and recovers a subset of weakly Pareto-efficient solutions of the coupled optimisation problems. The framework is further extended to a risk-sharing formulation, in which the objective is defined using conditional value-at-risk. Numerical simulations on an academic example demonstrate the method's behaviour and show that the resulting equilibrium partition set contains several admissible trade-offs between the competing objectives. The results provide a tractable approach for analysing competition, coalition formation, and risk-aware pricing in multi-retailer demand-side management systems.

</details>


### [142] [Infinite-Horizon Inverse Linear-Quadratic Differential Games with State- and Control-Dependent Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17939)
*Lucas Günther,Karl Handwerker,Felix Thömmes,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解具有状态和控制依赖噪声的N人无限时域线性二次微分博弈逆问题的方法，通过耦合随机代数Riccati方程的核表示，刻画了与观测到的均衡轨迹一致的所有成本函数参数组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机微分博弈中，从观测到的均衡行为反推参与者的成本函数是一个重要但具有挑战性的问题。现有方法难以处理状态和控制均含噪声的情形，因此需要发展新的理论工具。

Method: 推导了线性反馈纳什均衡存在的充要条件（耦合随机代数Riccati方程），并引入核表示法来显式刻画所有与观测轨迹一致的成本参数组合，从而求解逆问题。

Result: 得到了描述逆问题解集的核表示形式，数值结果验证了该方法的有效性，并揭示了逆问题本身存在内在的模糊性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功解决了带有状态和控制依赖噪声的LQ微分博弈逆问题，为从行为数据反推博弈结构提供了理论基础和计算工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a method to solve the inverse problem for N-player infinite-horizon linear-quadratic (LQ) differential games with state- and control-dependent noise. For this stochastic setting, we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for linear feedback Nash equilibria, which take the form of coupled stochastic algebraic Riccati equations. We then derive a kernel representation of these equations to explicitly characterize the set of all cost function parameter combinations across players that are consistent with observed equilibrium trajectories, thereby solving the associated inverse problem. Numerical results illustrate the approach and confirm the theoretical findings, highlighting the inherent ambiguity of the inverse problem.

</details>


### [143] [Policy Iteration for Linear-Quadratic Stochastic Differential Games with State- and Control-Dependent Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17940)
*Karl Handwerker,Felix Thömmes,Lucas Günther,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于具有状态和控制依赖噪声的随机微分博弈的新型顺序策略迭代方法，保证均方稳定性，并推导出在纳什均衡点处的Fréchet导数闭式表达，给出局部线性收敛的充分条件，同时提出基于同伦的初始化方法解决初始稳定解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统策略迭代方法在处理带有状态和控制依赖噪声的随机微分博弈时难以保证稳定性，且缺乏对局部收敛行为的理论分析，同时寻找初始稳定策略困难。

Method: 提出一种保持均方稳定的顺序策略迭代方法，推导了策略迭代映射在纳什均衡点的Fréchet导数闭式表达，分析误差传播机制，并引入基于同伦的初始化策略以获得初始稳定解。

Result: 得到了控制依赖噪声、策略评估敏感性和更新顺序对局部误差传播的影响机制，给出了局部线性收敛的显式充分条件，并通过数值算例验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅保证迭代过程的良定性，还提供了对收敛行为的深入理论理解，结合同伦初始化有效解决了初始稳定策略的获取难题。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel sequential policy iteration (PI) method for stochastic differential games with state- and control-dependent noise. The updates preserve mean-square stability, so that the iteration is well posed. We further derive a closed-form expression for the Fréchet derivative of the sequential PI map at a Nash equilibrium. The resulting characterization reveals how control-dependent noise, policy-evaluation sensitivity, and update ordering govern local error propagation, and yields explicit sufficient conditions for local linear convergence. Since finding an initial stabilizing solution is a major challenge in policy iteration, we also propose a homotopy-based initialization that ensures a valid starting point. The effectiveness of the proposed PI algorithm and the analytical results are verified through a numerical example.

</details>


### [144] [Extending and Unifying the Fundamental Tasks of Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18060)
*Dylan Hirsch,William Sharpless,Donggun Lee,Sylvia Herbert*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义可达避障（GRA）任务，统一并扩展了哈密顿-雅可比可达性（HJR）中的基本任务，能够分解复合任务的价值函数，并从偏微分方程角度展示了其理论与实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了统一和扩展哈密顿-雅可比可达性（HJR）中的基本任务，并支持更复杂的复合任务求解。

Method: 提出广义可达避障（GRA）任务，将其作为HJR框架下的通用原语，并通过分解复合任务的价值函数进行求解，同时从偏微分方程（PDE）角度分析其表达能力。

Result: GRA不仅统一了HJR中的经典任务，还扩展了其可解决的任务范围；能够分解时序逻辑等复合任务的价值函数；并能表示HJ-PDE的所有足够正则解。

Conclusion: GRA任务在HJR框架下具有重要的理论和实践价值，是处理复杂安全性和时序约束问题的有力工具。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce the generalized reach-avoid (GRA) task, which both extends and unifies the canonical tasks of Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability (HJR). We show that the GRA not only serves as a common primitive in this class of fundamental tasks, but also strictly extends the fundamental tasks that can be solved with HJR. Moreover, the GRA formulation enables one to compute the value functions of certain composite tasks, including ones from timed temporal logic, by decomposing the value function of the composite task into value functions of GRA tasks. We additionally show that the GRA is also a natural primitive to consider from a PDE perspective, as it can be used to represent all sufficiently regular solutions of the HJ-PDE that is canonical to HJR. Collectively, the results in this work show the theoretical and practical utility of this task within the increasingly important framework of HJR.

</details>


<div id='nlin.CD'></div>

### [145] [Set-Oriented Approach to the Analysis of Chaotic Itinerancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17905)
*Wojciech Jaworek,Paweł Pilarczyk*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图算法和马尔可夫链的数值方法，用于分析动力系统中的混沌游走（Chaotic Itinerancy, CI）现象，通过将状态空间离散化并构建有向图模型，识别不变集、计算稳态分布与局部熵，并应用于耦合逻辑映射系统和CML变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌游走现象在认知科学和神经动力学中有重要意义，但其检测缺乏系统性方法，因此需要一种能有效识别CI特性的计算框架。

Method: 将状态空间划分为有限网格，构建多值映射对应的加权有向图，利用强连通分量识别不变集，结合转移矩阵分析稳态分布与局部熵，进而判断CI特性。

Result: 成功开发出判断CI存在的算法，并在全局耦合逻辑映射和CML变体系统中实现了大范围参数下的数值验证。

Conclusion: 该图论与动力系统结合的方法为研究高维混沌系统中的复杂动态行为提供了高效且可扩展的工具，证实了所提算法在检测混沌游走方面的有效性。

Abstract: Chaotic itinerancy (CI), brought to attention, among others, by K. Ikeda, I. Tsuda and K. Kaneko in the early 1990s, is a phenomenon in which trajectories in a dynamical system experience periods of ordered motion near quasi-attractors interspersed with chaotic transitions between them. Possible maps in which CI was found include coupled map lattices (CML) and globally coupled one-dimensional chaotic maps (GCM). We study such maps using numerical methods and graph algorithms. Specifically, we partition the state space into a finite grid of compact subsets, and we represent the map using a multivalued mapping of grid elements. This mapping can be perceived as a directed graph, with grid elements as vertices and individual mappings between them as weighted edges. This setup provides a coarse view of global dynamics and opens the opportunity for using Markov chains and efficient graph algorithms to study dynamical features. In particular, invariant sets can be found by computing strongly connected components in the graph. Analysis of the transition matrix of the graph makes it possible to find its stationary distribution and to compute local entropy as a measure of expansion or instability in the system. Using these tools, we propose an algorithm for assessing whether a certain map possesses the CI property and show its application to dynamical systems: a globally coupled system of logistic maps and a variant of a CML system for which we conduct computations for a large range of parameters.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Incorporating Animal Movement into Continuous-Time Spatial Capture-Recapture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17046)
*Clara Panchaud,Ruth King,David Borchers,Hannah Worthington*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种将动物运动整合到空间捕获-重捕获（SCR）模型中的连续时间框架，通过模拟个体在离散化景观上的连续时间马尔可夫链运动和状态依赖的泊松检测过程，解决了传统SCR模型因忽略运动导致的种群数量估计偏差问题，并在美洲貂的数据分析中展示了对运动和密度的新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间捕获-重捕获（SCR）模型假设检测事件在给定活动中心的情况下时间上独立，忽略了个体运动引起的检测依赖性，可能导致种群大小估计偏差。为解决这一问题，需要显式建模个体运动以提高推断可靠性。

Method: 构建了一个连续时间框架，将个体运动建模为离散化景观上的连续时间马尔可夫链，检测视为状态依赖的泊松事件，形成马尔可夫调制标记泊松过程，实现基于似然的连续时间推断。

Result: 模拟研究表明，忽略运动依赖会导致种群数量正向偏倚，而新模型能恢复无偏估计并提供空间利用信息；应用于美洲貂的相机陷阱数据时，该模型揭示了运动模式与种群密度之间的新关系。

Conclusion: 显式建模个体运动对于空间捕获-重捕获研究中的可靠推断至关重要，所提框架提升了生态推断的准确性并拓展了对野生动物空间动态的理解。

Abstract: Estimation of wildlife population size and spatial dynamics is central to ecology and conservation. Spatial capture-recapture (SCR) models estimate abundance, using detections at sensors such as camera traps, by linking detection probability to the distance between detectors and latent individual activity centres. However, standard SCR formulations assume detections are conditionally independent over time given activity centres without explicitly modelling movement between detection events. This assumption can be problematic when individuals exhibit movement-driven dependence in detections, potentially leading to biased inference on population size. We address this important issue by developing a continuous-time framework that integrates movement into spatial capture-recapture. Individual movement is modelled as a continuous-time Markov chain over a discretised landscape, and detections arise as state-dependent Poisson events. This yields a Markov-modulated marked Poisson process representation, in which detections provide information about an individual's latent location at the time of observation and allow likelihood-based inference in continuous time. We show through simulation studies that ignoring movement-driven dependence can lead to positively biased estimates of population size, whereas the proposed model recovers unbiased estimates and provides additional inference on space use. An application to camera-trap data of American martens illustrates how the framework yields new insights into movement and density. These results demonstrate that explicitly modelling movement is critical for reliable inference in spatial capture-recapture studies.

</details>


### [147] [Identifying Model Quality Effects on User Engagement: A Within-Version Causal Estimator with Synthetic Data Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17187)
*John Tribbia*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的因果估计方法，利用单一模型版本中不同能力的非均匀质量提升来识别用户参与度变化的真实原因，并通过合成数据验证了该方法的有效性，显著优于传统朴素方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型更新后，用户参与度上升可能受多种因素干扰（如营销、媒体等），难以确定模型性能提升是否真正驱动了用户行为变化，因此需要一种能剥离混杂因素的因果推断方法。

Method: 利用同一模型版本中不同能力维度改进程度不均的现象（例如编程能力提升明显而写作能力提升较小），构建用户实际体验到的质量差异作为因果信号，结合误差修正调整（如变量误差中的衰减校正）进行因果效应估计。

Result: 在合成数据上，原始估计器恢复了81%到88%的真实效应，经误差校正后恢复率达101.7%（95%置信区间：91.5%~110.9%）；相比之下，朴素方法仅恢复63%（无版本控制）和62%（使用实时而非冻结的使用模式）。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效从混杂因素中分离出模型质量提升对用户参与的真实因果影响，具有良好的稳定性与精度，为LLM部署评估提供了可靠的因果分析框架。

Abstract: Every team building Large Language Models (LLMs) faces a core challenge: offline benchmarks show performance gains and user engagement rises post deployment, but isolating cause from effect remains difficult. Simultaneous marketing, media coverage, and seasonal demand obscure whether model updates truly drive engagement gains.
  This paper presents a novel causal estimation approach that leverages non uniform quality improvements across capabilities within a single model version. Because capabilities improve unevenly (e.g., strong gains in coding versus modest gains in writing), users experience varied quality depending on their task distribution. This variation in experienced quality provides causal signal for estimation.
  We validate this approach on synthetic data with known ground truth. The raw estimator recovers 81% to 88% of the true effect, with the remainder lost to measurement error in usage estimates. Applying an errors in variables disattenuation adjustment (using a test retest reliability ratio mean correlation of 0.861) corrects the estimate to 1.017 (bootstrapped 95% CI: 0.915 to 1.109).
  By contrast, naive methods fail significantly, recovering only 63% without version controls and 62% using real time rather than frozen usage patterns. Permutation tests confirm the framework distinguishes true causal effects from noise. Sensitivity analysis indicates recovery improves monotonically from 78% to 87% as pre period length increases from 3 to 7 weeks, highlighting a clear tradeoff between sample duration and estimator precision. Seed sensitivity tests further confirm stability across random draws.

</details>


### [148] [Nonparametric Estimation of Extropy, Rényi Extropy, and Tsallis Extropy: Almost Sure Convergence and Asymptotic Normality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17191)
*Amadou Diadie Ba*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于离散随机变量的非参数估计方法，用于extropy及其扩展形式（α-Rényi和α-Tsallis extropies），并通过理论和仿真验证了估计量的收敛性和渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为离散随机变量提供有效的extropy及其扩展形式的非参数估计方法，以支持其在实际应用中的使用。

Method: 采用基于插件（plug-in）的非参数估计方法，推导并分析了估计量的几乎必然收敛速度和渐近正态性。

Result: 建立了插件估计量的几乎必然收敛率和渐近正态性，并通过模拟研究验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的估计方法具有良好的统计性质，为extropy类度量在预测、风险评估和不确定性决策等实际场景中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper proposes a nonparametric estimation procedure for extropy and its extensions, namely the α-Rényi and α-Tsallis extropies, for finite discrete random variables. We establish almost sure rates of convergence and asymptotic normality for the plug-in estimators. The theoretical results are validated through a comprehensive simulation study. The findings provide a solid foundation for the use of extropy-based measures in practical applications, including forecasting, risk assessment, and decision-making under uncertainty.

</details>


### [149] [SDR Variance Estimates in Small Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17353)
*Eric Slud,Tim Trudell*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文探讨了连续差异复制（SDR）方法在复杂多阶段调查中方差估计的应用，重点分析其在小域估计中的偏差和变异性。研究表明，使用3到5个循环即可控制SDR估计的变异性，而更多的循环对减少小域偏差无显著作用。SDR会系统性地高估小域的平均方差，但通常增幅不超过15%，且在样本量较小时估计值极为不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估Successive Difference Replication (SDR) 方法在复杂多阶段调查特别是小域估计中的表现，解决缺乏其他方差估计方法对比的问题，并揭示其潜在偏差和变异性。

Method: 通过理论公式推导与模拟研究相结合的方法，系统分析SDR在不同设置下的方差估计表现，重点关注小域中方差高估的程度及其与属性均值、方差和调查权重排列模式的关系。

Result: 发现SDR在小域中会系统性地高估平均方差，增幅一般不超过15%（样本量为20时），但在特定情况下可能更高；同时SDR估计值具有极高变异性，标准差常远大于偏差本身；建议D取值为3至5即可有效控制变异性，更多循环无助于缓解偏差。

Conclusion: SDR是一种实用的方差估计方法，适用于复杂调查设计，但在小域应用时需注意其固有的方差高估和高度变异性问题；推荐使用3到5个循环以平衡精度与计算成本。

Abstract: Successive Difference Replication (SDR) is a replication based method of variance estimation introduced by Fay and Train (1995) for estimators based on complex multistage surveys, especially those including a final systematic sampling stage. The method has been used for many years as the primary variance-estimation methodology in large national household surveys administered by the Census Bureau, including the American Community Survey and also the Current Population Survey's monthly estimates based on self-representing strata. In settings where it is applied, generally no second method of variance estimation has been available, so the performance of SDR has been studied via simulation by various authors, for variances of survey totals and of nonlinear survey estimators. This paper begins with a thorough exposition of the SDR method and review of previously published results on the small-domain biases of SDR variance estimation. It is shown that the number D of cycles used in implementing SDR should be 3 or larger, in order to control the variability of SDR estimates, but need not be larger than 5. Beyond that, the value of D is virtually irrelevant to the occurrence of small-domain bias in SDR. The SDR method is shown via theoretical formulas and simulation to inflate average estimated variances in small domains by amounts that vary systematically with the patterns of attribute means and variances and survey weights in consecutively enumerated strata. The degree of average variance inflation is generally moderate, no more than 15 percent in domains with sample size 20, but can be larger in special settings. Moreover, SDR estimates are extremely variable in small domains, with standard deviations often far larger than any biases.

</details>


### [150] [Modified Bryson-Frazier Smoothing and Hyperparameter Learning for Temporal Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17595)
*Tom Colemont,Brecht Evens,Tjonnie G. F. Li,Frederik De Ceuster*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出使用改进的Bryson-Frazier（MBF）平滑器替代Rauch-Tung-Striebel（RTS）平滑器，用于一维高斯过程回归的状态空间表示，以避免协方差矩阵求逆带来的数值不稳定性，同时降低计算成本和内存需求，并支持高效的核超参数学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTS平滑器在处理预测状态协方差矩阵时需要进行矩阵求逆，可能导致病态条件和数值不稳定问题，限制了其在高斯过程回归中的可靠性和效率。

Method: 采用改进的Bryson-Frazier（MBF）平滑器进行状态空间形式的高斯过程回归，避免直接求逆协方差矩阵，并利用中间计算量高效计算负对数边缘似然的梯度，实现超参数学习。

Result: MBF平滑器与RTS平滑器计算出的后验分布相同，但更具数值稳定性，且计算效率更高；中间变量可重用于梯度计算，显著降低了超参数学习的额外开销。

Conclusion: MBF平滑器为一维高斯过程回归提供了一种统一、高效且数值稳健的推断与超参数学习方法。

Abstract: One-dimensional Gaussian processes with stationary, integrable kernel functions admit exact or arbitrarily accurate state-space representations, enabling linear-time inference through Kalman filtering and Rauch-Tung-Striebel (RTS) smoothing. However, the RTS smoother requires inversion of predicted state covariance matrices, which can become ill-conditioned and may therefore lead to numerical instabilities. In this work, we revisit the modified Bryson-Frazier (MBF) smoother as an alternative to the RTS smoother for Gaussian process regression in its state-space representation. In addition to reducing computational cost and memory requirements, the MBF smoother computes the same posterior distributions as the RTS smoother while avoiding the problematic covariance matrix inversion and the associated numerical instabilities. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the intermediate quantities computed by the MBF smoother can be reused to compute gradients of the negative log marginal likelihood, enabling kernel hyperparameter learning with minimal additional cost. Together, these results establish the MBF smoother as a unified and numerically robust approach to inference and kernel hyperparameter learning for one-dimensional Gaussian process regression.

</details>


### [151] [Blinded sample size review for McNemar's test based on primary and surrogate endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17784)
*Markus Schepers,Werner Brannath,Esther Hoffmann,Julia Stingl,Irene Schmidtmann*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于McNemar检验的配对二元主要和次要短期替代终点的盲法样本量重估策略，应用于儿童青光眼临床试验，通过条件效能和替代终点信息实现样本量调整，同时控制I类错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受一项关于儿童青光眼的前瞻性随机临床试验启发，需在中期分析时根据已有数据调整样本量，以提高统计效能并保证试验的灵活性。

Method: 推导了在中期分析给定主要终点时McNemar检验的条件效能表达式，并提出样本量重估规则；对于仅有短期替代终点的患者，引入基于替代终点的重估方法，利用替代与主要结局之间的转换率进行估计。

Result: 该方法能有效保持I类错误率，同时根据预设目标效能动态选择第二阶段样本量；替代终点方法可通过子样本中双终点数据估计转换率并应用于重估。

Conclusion: 所提出的盲法样本量重估策略结合主要和替代终点信息，在保持统计严谨性的同时提高了临床试验设计的适应性和效率，适用于配对二元数据的场景。

Abstract: We develop blinded sample size re-estimation strategies for McNemar's test based on paired binary primary and secondary short-term surrogate endpoints. The development is motivated by a prospective randomized clinical trial on childhood glaucoma. A conditional power expression for McNemar's test given the primary endpoint at an interim analysis is derived and complemented by a sample size re-estimation rule. We show that this procedure preserves the type I error rate while allowing the second-stage sample size to be chosen to attain a prespecified target power. In the case where for some patients only a short-term surrogate endpoint is available at interim, we introduce a surrogate-based re-estimation approach that conditions on all possible numbers of primary-endpoint discordant pairs using transition rates from the surrogate to the primary outcome. We show how these transition rates can be estimated from data on a subsample for which both surrogate and primary endpoint are available. We derive the resulting surrogate endpoint-based conditional power and sample size rule and illustrate their use with the example of the motivating trial.

</details>


### [152] [Graph-Adaptive Horseshoe for Compositional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17858)
*Satabdi Saha,Christine B. Peterson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GRACE的全贝叶斯框架，用于成分数据回归中的变量选择和图结构自适应学习，通过新颖的系数重参数化和结构化horseshoe先验，在满足成分约束的同时实现稀疏性和图平滑性，并在口腔微生物组数据中成功识别与胰岛素抵抗相关的类群及其结果驱动的关系网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于固定的关联图（如系统发育或生态距离），无法反映与特定结果相关的特征关系，且难以处理成分数据的单位和约束及变量间依赖性，因此需要一种能自适应学习结果相关图结构并进行有效变量选择的方法。

Method: 提出GRACE模型：采用线性重参数化确保成分约束；引入结构化horseshoe先验实现稀疏与图平滑；利用scaled beta2先验自适应学习边权重以构建结果驱动的收缩图；设计结合椭圆切片采样的Gibbs采样器提升高维可扩展性。

Result: 模拟实验显示GRACE在预测精度和图恢复能力上优于现有方法，尤其在图误设情况下表现更优；应用于ORIGINS研究的口腔微生物组数据时，成功识别出与胰岛素抵抗相关的细菌类群，并生成了一个与系统发育或共现网络显著不同的结果驱动关系图。

Conclusion: 固定预测图虽有助于正则化，但未必能准确反映结果相关的特征关系，GRACE通过自适应学习结果驱动的图结构，为成分数据回归提供了更灵活、更具解释性的变量选择方法，强调了在成分数据分析中采用结果导向自适应方法的重要性。

Abstract: Compositional predictors, such as microbiome abundances, pose unique challenges in variable selection due to their unit-sum constraint and inherent dependencies. Existing approaches often rely on fixed association graphs derived from phylogenetic or ecological distances, which may not reflect outcome-relevant relationships. We propose GRACE (GRaph-Adaptive horseshoe for Compositional rEgression), a fully Bayesian framework that enforces compositional constraints, performs variable selection, and adaptively learns an outcome-driven shrinkage graph. GRACE achieves compositionality through a novel linear reparameterization of regression coefficients, while a structured horseshoe prior induces sparsity and smooths coefficients along the learned graph. Graph learning is accomplished via scaled beta2 priors on edge weights, providing both outcome-specific adaptation and posterior uncertainty quantification. We develop an efficient Gibbs sampler incorporating elliptical slice sampling to ensure scalability in high dimensions. Through extensive simulations, GRACE demonstrates competitive predictive accuracy and improved graph recovery compared with existing methods, particularly under graph misspecification. Application to oral microbiome data from the ORIGINS study identifies taxa associated with insulin resistance and yields an outcome-driven graph summarizing how those taxa relate to the outcome, a structure that differs substantially from phylogenetic or co-occurrence networks. These findings highlight that fixed predictor graphs useful for regularization may not faithfully represent outcome-relevant feature relationships, underscoring the need for adaptive, outcome-informed approaches in compositional regression.

</details>


### [153] [Transporting Trial Evidence Under Posterior Drift and Possible Hidden Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17999)
*Xilin Mao,Bosen Cui,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒后验漂移框架，用于在观察性研究中估计目标人群的平均处理效应，考虑了随机试验与观察性研究之间的条件效应不可迁移性和隐藏混杂因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机试验虽内部有效性高，但样本可能不代表目标人群；观察性研究更接近目标人群，但可能存在隐藏混杂。因此需要一种方法能在两者之间平衡并处理不确定性。

Method: 构建了一个以随机试验为锚点、观察性研究提供协变量分布的框架，引入源特异性漂移，并通过Rosenbaum型不确定性集和极小化极大最坏情况风险准则来估计漂移，适用于参数和非参数漂移类。

Result: 推导了辅助情形下的效率结果，建立了极小化极大估计量的一致收敛性和近最优性保证，在模拟和ACTG 175--WIHS应用中表现出比精确可迁移性分析更谨慎和可解释的结果。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效整合随机试验与观察性研究的信息，在存在隐藏混杂和条件效应不可迁移时仍可稳健估计目标人群的处理效应。

Abstract: Randomized trials provide internally valid treatment-effect evidence, but trial participants may not represent the target population. In contrast, observational studies are often closer to the target population, but their treatment assignment may be affected by possible hidden confounding. We develop a robust posterior-drift framework for estimating the average treatment effect in an observational target population when exact conditional-effect transportability may fail. The framework represents observational conditional potential-outcome regressions as their randomized-trial counterparts plus source-specific drifts. The randomized trial serves as an internally valid anchor, while the observational study supplies the target covariate distribution and partial information about the target causal contrast. To account for possible hidden confounding, we consider a Rosenbaum-type uncertainty set induced by a sensitivity parameter on the generalized propensity score and estimate the drift through a minimax worst-case risk criterion. We derive efficiency results in auxiliary regimes, establish uniform concentration and near-optimality guarantees for the minimax estimator, and handle general parametric and smooth nonparametric drift classes. Simulations and an ACTG 175--WIHS application show that the proposed analysis yields more cautious and interpretable target-population effect estimates than exact-transportability analyses.

</details>


### [154] [Scalable Heteroskedastic Gaussian Process Models for Large Inhomogeneous Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18018)
*K. Potter,K. R. Moran,R. Ulrich,D. C. Stenning,D. Bingham,L. Castro,G. Wilson,C. A. Maldonado*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Heteroskedastic Normalized Vecchia (HetNV)的高斯过程回归框架，用于处理输入依赖的观测噪声，通过Vecchia似然近似与残差平滑估计噪声方差，在保持均值预测精度的同时更准确地捕捉输入相关的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的高斯过程模型通常假设同方差噪声，难以有效建模输入依赖的异方差噪声；现有方法在大规模数据下计算复杂度高或需复杂的潜变量推断，因此需要一种可扩展且无需潜变量推理的高效方法。

Method: HetNV结合了归一化输入上的Vecchia似然近似与基于残差的非参数方差估计：首先使用带观测特定nugget方差的Vecchia GP估计潜在均值，然后通过对考虑克里金不确定性和当前nugget估计的平方残差伪响应取对数并进行稳定化处理，再用LOESS（一维）或薄板样条GAM（二维）平滑得到对数噪声方差；算法交替更新均值和方差，避免对方差过程进行潜变量推断。

Result: 在固定邻域大小m和样本量n下，每次迭代的主要成本为O(nm²)，具有良好的可扩展性；模拟实验表明，相比同方差Vecchia模型，HetNV能更准确恢复输入依赖的不确定性，同时保持有竞争力的均值预测性能，并在二维情形下进一步提升均值估计效果；在航天器等离子体测量数据中的应用显示，局部自适应的不确定性估计显著影响大型异方差场景下的信号检测决策。

Conclusion: HetNV提供了一种高效、可扩展且无需潜变量推断的方法来建模输入依赖的异方差高斯过程，能够在大规模异方差数据中实现精确的均值与不确定性联合估计，适用于科学实际应用中的复杂噪声结构。

Abstract: We introduce Heteroskedastic Normalized Vecchia Gaussian Processes (HetNV), a scalable framework for Gaussian process regression with input-dependent observation noise. HetNV combines Vecchia likelihood approximations on normalized inputs with residual-based nonparametric variance estimation. The latent mean is estimated via a Vecchia GP with observation-specific nugget variances, while the log noise variance is obtained by smoothing stabilized log-squared residual pseudo-responses that account for kriging uncertainty and current nugget estimates, using LOESS in one dimension and thin plate spline generalized additive models in two dimensions. The method alternates between mean and variance updates, avoiding latent-variable inference for the variance process. For fixed neighborhood size ($m$) and number of observations ($n$), the dominant per-iteration cost is the Vecchia update, scaling as $O(nm^2)$. Simulation studies show improved recovery of input-dependent uncertainty relative to homoskedastic Vecchia models while maintaining competitive mean prediction accuracy, with additional gains in two-dimensional mean estimation. An application to spacecraft plasma measurements demonstrates how locally adaptive uncertainty estimates influence downstream signal-detection decisions in large, noisy, heteroskedastic settings.

</details>


<div id='nlin.AO'></div>
