### [155] [Phase-based spatial ordinal patterns for characterizing oscillatory dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17196)
*Robison J. Santos-Silva,Bruno R. R. Boaretto,Thiago L. Prado,Roberto C. Budzinski*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于空间序数模式的框架，用于表征振荡系统的时空动力学，能够捕捉相位梯度和同步簇，并通过空间排列熵量化时空模式多样性，有效识别瞬态动态和状态转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有瞬态动力学的振荡系统中，识别并追踪时空模式的演化具有挑战性，尤其是在神经群体和工程网络等复杂系统中。

Method: 利用相位而非振幅构建空间序数模式，引入处理近似相等相位的新模式，定义空间排列熵以量化每时刻的时空模式多样性。

Result: 该方法能区分具有相同全局同步水平但空间结构不同的相位锁定状态，刻画部分同步状态，并在合成振荡网络和人脑静息态EEG数据中成功识别不同条件和动态 regime。

Conclusion: 所提框架为分析振荡系统中的时空组织提供了有效工具，适用于检测瞬态动态和复杂同步结构，在理论和实际应用中均有潜力。

Abstract: The emergence of organized spatiotemporal patterns is ubiquitous in oscillatory systems, from neural populations to engineered networks. Identifying these patterns and tracking how they evolve over time remains challenging, particularly when systems exhibit transient dynamics. Here, we introduce a framework based on spatial ordinal patterns to characterize the spatiotemporal dynamics of oscillatory systems. Our approach acts directly on the phase rather than the amplitude, with additional patterns introduced to account for near-equal phases. This symbolic representation encodes local spatial ordering relations, capturing both phase gradients and synchronized clusters within a single framework. From this construction, we define a spatial permutation entropy that quantifies the diversity of spatiotemporal patterns at each point in time, enabling the detection of transient dynamics and regime transitions as they occur. We show that this approach distinguishes phase-locked states with identical levels of global synchronization but distinct spatial organization and also characterizes partially synchronized states. We demonstrate the method on synthetic oscillator networks across multiple spatiotemporal regimes, and on resting-state EEG recordings from human volunteers, where it distinguishes different conditions within individual volunteers.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Critical couplings of two dimensional Ising model on various lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16949)
*Sh. Khachatryan,A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的费米场方法，利用Kac-Ward表示研究二维Ising模型在多种平面晶格上的临界性质，成功复现了已知的精确临界耦合，并给出了各向异性kagomé和dice晶格的临界条件及低能谱特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了统一描述不同几何结构晶格上Ising模型的临界行为，发展一种基于费米场的普适方法。

Method: 采用Kac-Ward表示法，将格点链路上的费米场与路径转向相位结合，通过动量空间矩阵的行列式表达配分函数，并分析其零点以确定激发谱和临界耦合。

Result: 在方格、蜂窝、三角、kagomé和dice晶格上均复现了已知的精确临界耦合；获得了各向异性kagomé晶格的完整临界面和低能谱方程；构建了dice晶格的费米作用量并导出了各向异性临界条件；在各向同性情况下，低能谱呈现相对论性有质量形式，质量在特定临界耦合处消失。

Conclusion: 该费米场构造为具有不同局部几何和配位数的Ising模型提供了紧凑且普适的临界性和低能激发描述框架。

Abstract: We develop a unified fermionic-field formulation of the two-dimensional Ising model on several planar lattices using the Kac--Ward representation. Grassmann fields are associated with directed lattice links, while the turning of a fermionic trajectory at a lattice vertex is encoded by the corresponding Kac--Ward phase factor. Within this approach the partition function is expressed through the determinant of a finite-dimensional momentum-space matrix, whose zeros determine the excitation spectrum and the critical coupling.
  We apply the method to the regular square, honeycomb, triangular, kagomé, and dual kagomé (dice or $T_3$) lattices. In all cases the known exact critical couplings are reproduced. Particular attention is given to the anisotropic kagomé lattice, for which the fermionic determinant yields the complete critical surface and the low-energy spectral equation. We also construct the fermionic action for the dual kagomé lattice and derive its anisotropic critical condition. In the isotropic dice model the spectrum reduces at low energy and momentum to a relativistic massive form, with the mass vanishing at $\cosh(2J_c)=(1+\sqrt3)/2$. The results demonstrate that the same fermionic construction provides a compact description of criticality and low-energy excitations for Ising models on lattices with different local geometries and coordination numbers.

</details>


### [157] [Incidence-based random walks on simplicial complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17229)
*C. T. Martínez-Martínez,Francisco J. Sevilla*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于边的随机游走模型，研究在二维随机单纯复形中高阶连接性对谱和传输特性的影响，发现中间面密度下会出现显著的传输瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶连接性如何影响网络中的传输与谱性质，特别是在保持成对连接结构不变的情况下。

Method: 构建一个由顶点和三角面共同介导的边上的随机游走模型，通过调节混合参数q控制转移算子，并分析其结构可观测量、谱松弛、稳态局域化和首达时间传输。

Result: 部分面保留导致异质性高阶连接，在中等面密度时出现非单调行为，表现为第二大特征值模、逆参与比和平均首达时间均达到峰值，且首达时间分布显示长轨迹概率增加。

Conclusion: 该模型为研究高阶连接性如何重塑超越成对网络动力学的谱与传输特性提供了一个简单而有效的框架。

Abstract: We introduce an incidence-based random walk on the edges of a random two-dimensional simplicial complex with a complete $1$-skeleton and independently retained triangular faces. The dynamics combine two transport channels, one mediated by vertices and the other by triangular faces, through an effective transition operator controlled by a mixing parameter $q$. This construction isolates the effects of higher-order connectivity without modifying the underlying pairwise support of the walk. We characterize the model through structural observables, spectral relaxation, stationary localization, and first-passage transport. Our results show that partial face retention generates heterogeneous higher-order connectivity, giving rise to a pronounced transport bottleneck at intermediate face densities. In this regime, the second-largest eigenvalue modulus, the inverse participation ratio of the stationary distribution, and the mean first-passage time all exhibit non-monotonic behavior, reaching their largest values at intermediate face densities. The corresponding first-passage-time distributions reveal an enhanced probability of unusually long trajectories. Together, these results establish a simple framework for investigating how heterogeneous higher-order connectivity reshapes spectral and transport properties beyond pairwise network dynamics.

</details>


### [158] [Spontaneous symmetry-breaking in equilibrium tree-packing configurations of a kinetically constrained cubic-lattice system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17308)
*Hai-Jun Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了立方晶格中由动力学约束引起的热力学相变，使用K=2的Fredrikson-Anderson自旋模型，发现存在连续的气-晶相变，并确定了临界化学势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索动力学约束如何在三维系统中诱导出热力学相变，特别是在二维中不存在该现象的情况下理解维度的作用。

Method: 采用K=2的Fredrikson-Anderson模型，在立方晶格上分析微观构型的动力学连通性，并引入全局约束下的平衡配分函数描述可到达的构型空间。

Result: 发现了立方系统中连续的气-晶相变，确定临界化学势μ* ≈ -3.252，此时占据位点开始偏好两个嵌套立方子晶格之一；该相变在二维方格中不存在。

Conclusion: 动力学约束可在三维中引发热力学相变，且维度对相变的存在起关键作用，揭示了树状构型堆积与子晶格有序之间的关联。

Abstract: We explore kinetic-constraint induced thermodynamic phase transition in the cubic lattice, employing the Fredrikson-Anderson spin model with hyperparameter $K=2$ as a representative kinetic system. Each lattice site may flip its binary occupation state if at most one of its six nearest neighbors is currently occupied. The whole set of microscopic configurations that are kinetically connected with the fully empty one is described by an equilibrium partition function with a single global constraint, that is, the occupied sites do not form closed loops but instead organize into different tree components in the lattice. We discover a continuous thermodynamic gas--crystal phase transition in the cubic system and determine the critical chemical potential $μ^* \approx -3.252$, at which the occupied sites of the equilibrium tree-packing configurations start to prefer one of the two nested cubic sublattices. This thermodynamic phase transition is absent in the two-dimensional square lattice.

</details>


### [159] [Conditional-path Monte Carlo for rare stochastic dynamics on networks: Details and derivations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17511)
*Thomas Barthel,Jiazheng Sun,Jhao-Hong Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了条件路径蒙特卡洛（CPMC）方法，用于高效模拟随机网络动力学中的稀有宏观事件，如流行病爆发等，克服了传统方法的多种局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准前向时间算法、分裂方法和过渡路径采样在模拟稀有事件时面临拒绝率高、权重退化等问题，亟需更高效的方法。

Method: 采用基于非局域Swendsen-Wang类簇更新的技术，直接在全系统轨迹上操作，定义联合路径-图概率权重，并推导保证细致平衡的转移与均匀化求和规则，结合动态规划实现复杂边界条件。

Result: 成功应用于SIS模型，实现了无拒绝采样，有效避免了锁雪崩并保持了流行病主干的结构流动性，且通过小规模网络的精确解验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: CPMC框架为稀有事件模拟提供了高效、严谨的新工具，具有良好的可扩展性和并行化潜力，适用于多种复杂系统动力学研究。

Abstract: The simulation of rare macroscopic events in stochastic network dynamics, such as widespread epidemic outbreaks, cascading failures in communication networks, or the escape from metastable states in many-body systems, is severely hindered by methodological challenges like catastrophic rejection rates, weight degeneracy, genealogical correlations, and critical slowing down inherent to standard forward-time algorithms, splitting methods, and transition-path sampling. Conditional-path Monte Carlo (CPMC) overcomes these limitations by employing non-local Swendsen-Wang-like cluster updates that operate directly on full-system trajectories. Serving as the technical companion to [Sun, Moody, and Barthel, arXiv:2608.16171], this paper provides the rigorous mathematical foundations and algorithmic details underlying the CPMC framework. We formally define the joint path-graph probability weights and derive the transition and uniformization sum rules that guarantee detailed balance. Applying the framework to susceptible-infectious-susceptible (SIS) models, we systematically construct and optimize single-node and edge graph vertex sets specifically designed to prevent lock avalanches and maintain the structural mobility of the epidemic trunk. Furthermore, we detail a dynamic programming scheme to exactly implement complex boundary conditions - including patient-zero and macroscopic outbreak-size constraints - enabling the rejection-free generation of valid trajectories. Finally, we assess the computational complexity of the algorithm, describe parallelization strategies, and validate CPMC against exact solutions for dynamics on small networks.

</details>


### [160] [Frustration without Glass in Non-Abelian Simplicial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17817)
*Xuanhua Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种非阿贝尔结构一致性的理论框架，用于描述动态变换位于单纯网络链路上的系统，并通过SU(2)模型模拟发现高无序相中存在非玻璃态的关联规范液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一个能够描述具有链路动力学的单纯网络系统中结构一致性的非阿贝尔理论，并探究无序对全局兼容性的影响。

Method: 基于规范协变性和plaquette holonomy构建非阿贝尔理论，在具有淬火随机plaquette耦合的完全单纯2-复形上进行SU(2)模型的平行回火模拟。

Result: 模拟显示随着系统尺寸增大，均匀兼容性Mp减小，相邻关联权重积分Iadj保持有限值，复制重叠宽度趋近数值噪声基底且分布变窄，未见热力学复制对称性破缺迹象。

Conclusion: 高度挫败导致一种非玻璃态的关联规范液体，其中个体对关联被几何稀释，但整体仍保留有限的集成关联权重。

Abstract: We formulate a non-Abelian theory of structural consistency for systems in which dynamical transformations reside on the links of a simplicial network. Gauge covariance follows from freedom to choose local representation frames, while plaquette holonomies quantify the incompatibility of alternative paths. For an SU(2) model on the complete simplicial $2$-complex with quenched random plaquette couplings, parallel-tempering simulations reveal a continuous disorder-driven loss of global compatibility. In the high-disorder phase, the uniform compatibility $M_P$ decreases with system size, the integrated adjacent correlation weight $\mathcal I_{\mathrm{adj}}$ remains finite value. Moreover, the connected replica-overlap width approaches the numerical noise floor and its distribution narrows, providing no evidence for thermodynamic replica-symmetry breaking. Dense frustration therefore produces a non-glassy correlated gauge liquid in which individual pair correlations are geometrically diluted while a finite integrated correlation weight survives.

</details>


### [161] [Heat capacity as a marker for shape and jamming transitions in active systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17903)
*Ion Santra*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了活性粒子系统中持久性对非平衡态热容的影响，发现其在相变区域表现出显著的量热特征。通过格点Run-and-Tumble动力学模型，作者计算了温度扰动下的过剩热量，揭示了单粒子边界累积和多粒子集群重组对应的热响应峰。结果表明，非平衡热容可作为探测非平衡相变的有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨活性物质系统中持久性驱动的非平衡相变如何在热力学响应中体现，寻找可用于实验检测的量热信号。

Method: 采用符合局域细致平衡的格点run-and-tumble动力学模型，计算小温度扰动后的过剩热量，从而得到非平衡热容，并分析单粒子与多粒子系统的热响应行为。

Result: 单粒子系统在持久性导致形状转变的区域出现热容最大值；在具有排他性相互作用的周期格点系统中，集群重组引起热响应峰值；时间对称部分的跃迁速率也影响结果，且该效应可通过交流量热法在实验中观测。

Conclusion: 非平衡热容可作为探测活性物质中非平衡相变的有力工具，为实验识别此类相变提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Persistence influences the stationary states of active particles, producing boundary accumulation at the single-particle level, and clustering or jamming in interacting systems. These features disappear as the persistence decreases and the system approaches a more passive-like stationary state. We show that these transitions have a distinct calorimetric signature. Using a lattice run-and-tumble dynamics, consistent with local detailed balance, we compute the nonequilibrium heat capacity from the excess heat released following a small temperature perturbation. For a single particle confined between reflecting boundaries, the heat capacity develops a maximum in the persistence regime corresponding to shape transition. Adding an exclusion interaction to the active particles on a periodic lattice, the reorganization of jammed clusters produces a corresponding peak in the thermal response. We also discuss the impact of the time-symmetric part of the transition rates, and show the possibility of seeing the same signatures of heat response in experiments by AC calorimetry. Our results show that nonequilibrium heat capacities can serve as calorimetric probes of nonequilibrium phase transitions.

</details>


### [162] [Critical behavior and crossover scaling in the Light-Heavy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18016)
*Shilpa Prakash,Mustansir Barma,Kabir Ramola*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻-重（LH）模型在不同驱动强度下的动力学行为，发现未缩放（uLH）状态下存在由多模态驱动的涨落主导有序相（FDPO），而在缩放（sLH）状态下转变为单主模控制并出现反常长程关联；通过类比sABC模型，推导出两点半函数的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解粒子与表面倾斜耦合系统中在不同驱动条件下的相变与关联行为，特别是在临界与非临界区域的动力学演化机制。

Method: 采用理论建模分析LH模型的两种极限（未缩放与缩放），结合数值与解析方法，利用标度函数连接临界与非临界区域，并借鉴sABC模型的方法推导关联函数。

Result: 在uLH regime中发现FDPO相及多模态动力学和动态簇形成；在sLH regime中观察到单主模主导动力学和1/√L形式的反常长程关联。

Conclusion: LH模型在不同参数区域展现出丰富动力学行为：未缩放时表现为多模态、FDPO特征，缩放后转为单模主导并出现解析可描述的反常长程关联。

Abstract: The Light-Heavy (LH) model involves two species of particles (light and heavy) coupled with a fluctuating surface (described by tilts). The dynamics include the inherent diffusion of the particles (or tilts) as well as the drive provided by the tilts (or particles). When the two are of similar magnitude, the system lies in the unscaled (uLH) regime, while a significantly weaker drive leads to the scaled (sLH) regime. In the unscaled limit, the model exhibits an order-disorder transition characterized by the fluctuation-dominated phase ordering (FDPO). In this state, interestingly the dynamics is driven by multiple modes, giving rise to dynamic clusters. Away from the critical regime the disordered phase retains vestiges of FDPO behavior on length scales smaller than the correlation length. We examine this local FDPO-like behavior by using a scaling function that links the off-critical and critical regimes. We next turn to the scaled model and show that the multi-mode dynamics present in the unscaled regime is replaced by dynamics that is effectively controlled by a single dominant mode in the scaled regime. Concurrently, the two-point correlations change from the $\mathcal{O}(1)$ FDPO form to an anomalous long-range form that decays as $1/\sqrt{L}$. Drawing on the analogy with the sABC model, where similar anomalous correlations appear at criticality, we derive an analytical expression for the two-point correlation function using the same approach used for that model.

</details>

### [163] [Validating direct solvers for Newton's gravitational N-body problem, and the systematic comparison between IEEE floating point and Posits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17032)
*Simon Portegies Zwart*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了任意精度算术、IEEE-754浮点格式（fp16, bfp16, fp32, fp64, fp128）和两种Posits实现（type III unum）在求解牛顿混沌N体问题中的精度与速度表现。结果显示，低精度格式（如fp16、bfp16和Posits<16,1>）精度不足；单精度格式可用于统计系综计算但个体强相互作用误差较大；64位格式中，fp64与Posits<64,3>均存在困难，其中Universal版Posits精度接近fp64但速度慢一个数量级以上，CPPPosits速度相当但误差大一个数量级且出现结果漂移。此外，Posits在移动参考系下积分动力学系统时难以满足伽利略不变性。结论是当前实现的Posits并非积分混沌或刚性微分方程（如牛顿运动方程）的理想替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找比IEEE-754浮点数更优的数值表示方法，以提升在混沌和刚性常微分方程（如牛顿N体问题）数值积分中的精度与效率，并评估新兴的Posits格式是否可作为双精度浮点（fp64）的有效替代。

Method: 通过任意精度计算作为基准，系统性地对比多种数值格式（包括不同精度的IEEE-754浮点数、任意精度算术及两种Posits实现）在求解牛顿混沌N体问题时的表现。使用硬件/编译器支持的fp64实现作为性能参照，其余采用软件实现。评估指标包括计算精度（与基准的偏差）和运行速度，并特别测试了算法在移动参考系下的行为（伽利略不变性检验）。

Result: 1) 半精度格式（fp16, bfp16, Posits<16,1>）精度严重不足；2) 单精度格式（fp32, Posits<32,2>）可用于统计分析但个体轨迹误差大；3) 64位格式中：Universal Posits精度接近fp64但极慢（至少慢一个数量级）；CPPPosits速度快但误差显著更大（约大一个数量级，峰值超两个数量级），导致结果空间漂移和无法解析近距离相遇事件；4) 所有Posits和fp64在移动参考系测试中均表现不佳，挑战伽利略不变性。

Conclusion: 尽管Posits在理论上具有优势，但其当前的软件实现形式在解决混沌或刚性常微分方程（如牛顿运动方程）时，无法在精度和速度上同时匹敌成熟的fp64实现。因此，在现有技术水平下，Posits尚不能作为fp64的理想替代方案。

Abstract: We present a systematic comparison between arbitrary precise arithmetic and integration, IEEE-754 compliant floating point arithmetic (fp16, bfp16, fp32, double precision fp64, and quadruple precision fp128), and two implementations of Posits (type III unum) for solving Newton's chaotic N-body problem. Each implementation is benchmarked with arbitrary precise calculations to objectively evaluate their performance in precision as well as speed. We rely on hardware and compiler implementations for fp64, and software implementations for arbitrary-precision arithmetic and Posits. Half precision arithmetic (fp16, bfp16, and Posits$<16,1>$) are insufficiently precise for solving Newton's equations of motion. Single precision (fp32, and Posits$<32,2>$) could be used for statistical ensemble calculations, but lead to relatively large errors in any individual strong encounter. All 64-bit implementations fp64 as well as Posits (Posits$<64,3>$) experience difficulty in our tests. One of the implementations of Posits (Universal) gives precision comparable to fp64 but is slow (by at least an orders of magnitude compared to fp64 after correcting for the more efficient hardware support for the latter). The other (CPPPosits) has a speed comparable to fp64 but has systematically larger errors (by about an order of magnitude compared to fp64 with excesses exceeding two orders of magnitude). As a consequence, this implementation leads to a systematic drift in the result space and has difficulty resolving close encounters. Posits and fp64 have difficulty when integrating a dynamical system in a moving reference frame; testing Galileo invariancy. In their current implementation, Posits do not seem to be the ideal alternative for fp64 when integrating chaotic or stiff ordinary differential equations, such as Newton's equations of motion.

</details>


### [164] [Agentic Porting, Construction and Initial Verification and Validation of Libraries within the Open Source Unified TRAnsient Multi-Phase Advanced Reactor simulation Kit (Outram Park) Part I: Thermal Hydraulics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17504)
*Theodore Kay Chen Ong,Ethan Yew Hoe Wong,Sicong Xiao*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人类参与的智能体方法，将多个开源库移植到Rust语言中，用于构建Outram Park中的模块。重点是将OpenFOAM库移植为Outram-Foam，并开发了TAMPINES项目下的两相均相平衡（HEM）求解器和蒸汽物性库。初步验证结果显示与文献数据吻合良好，表明该方法在加速开源模拟代码开发方面具有潜力，但人类专家在确保代码有效性方面仍不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代码移植和开发过程效率较低，尤其是在需要高可靠性的核反应堆瞬态多相模拟中。通过引入智能体辅助的自动代码生成并结合人类专家的监督，旨在提升开发效率，加快开源仿真工具链的建设。

Method: 采用智能体驱动的自动化代码移植方法，将C++编写的OpenFOAM库逐步转换为Rust语言的Outram-Foam库；结合人类专家进行关键环节的验证与修正；进一步开发基于Rust的1D HEM求解器和tampines-steam-tables物性库，并在Outram Park框架内集成与测试。

Result: Outram-Foam对腔体流动和Sod激波管案例的模拟结果与文献值基本一致；tampines-steam-tables与Moody的HEM图表吻合良好；1D HEM求解器在Edwards吹扫案例中完成初步验证；使用Claude Code提升了开发效率，但仍需人类专家介入以保证正确性。

Conclusion: 智能体辅助的代码移植方法显著提升了开发效率，在构建可靠的开源多相流模拟工具方面展现出巨大潜力，但当前阶段仍依赖人类专家进行关键验证与调整，未来需加强验证与确认工作以提升自动化程度。

Abstract: Agentic porting of multiple open-source libraries into Rust, with human in the loop, has been performed for construction of modules within the Open-source Unified TRAnsient Multi-Phase Advanced Reactor simulation Kit (Outram Park). With this new methodology, verification and validation with human expertise, rather than code generation has become the bottleneck in developing reliable simulation codes. In this work, we present the porting of OpenFOAM libraries into the Outram-Foam Rust libraries, their preliminary verification and validation (V\&V) efforts, and their subsequent use in the development of open-source two-phase homogeneous-equilibrium (HEM) choked-flow solvers for the Thermo-hydraulic AI Multi-Phase INtegrated Emulator System (TAMPINES) libraries within Outram Park such as tampines-steam-tables. Preliminary V\&V efforts of Outram-Foam show that the cavity and Sod shock tube cases agree reasonably well with literature values. Moreover, the preliminary development of tampines-steam-tables shows good agreement with Moody's HEM charts. Thereafter, the 1D HEM solver, developed agentically, is presented and preliminarily validated against the Edwards blowdown case. Productivity increases were observed with the use of Claude Code, but domain expertise supplied by human experts remains critically required to ensure the generated code can solve the problem effectively. Further work remains to be done in V\&V, but the agentic coding methodology in this paper demonstrates great potential to speed up production and development of open-source libraries such as Outram Park.

</details>


### [165] [Probabilistic Inverse Modeling of Contaminant Transport via a Conditioned-on-Design Bayesian Physics Informed Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17591)
*Milad Panahi,Giovanni Michele Porta,Monica Riva,Alberto Guadagnini*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于设计条件的贝叶斯物理信息神经网络（CoDe-BPINN），用于从稀疏实验数据中推断非均质多孔介质中反应性输运模型的参数，并实现不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决因强非线性、空间异质性和数据稀缺导致的反应性输运反问题中参数难以准确推断的问题。

Method: 结合域分解的PINN求解器与贝叶斯推断网络，通过最大化物理信息引导的ELBO进行训练，实现对模型参数条件分布的学习。

Result: 在污染物通过多层多孔柱的实验中成功重建了浓度场，揭示了吸附容量随流速和孔隙率非线性下降的关系，并发现了吸附亲和性与容量之间的强负相关性。

Conclusion: CoDe-BPINN为数据稀缺条件下的反应性输运反问题提供了鲁棒的参数推断与不确定性量化框架。

Abstract: We address the inverse problem of reactive transport in heterogeneous porous media, where unknown model parameters must be inferred from sparse experimental observations. The problem is complicated by strong nonlinearities, spatial heterogeneity, and limited data availability. We propose a Conditioned-on-Design Bayesian Physics-Informed Neural Network (CoDe-BPINN), which combines a domain-decomposed PINN solver with a Bayesian inference network that learns the conditional distribution of model parameters given experimental design variables. The framework is trained by maximizing a physics-informed Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO), enabling simultaneous reconstruction of spatiotemporal concentration fields, probabilistic parameter estimation, and uncertainty quantification. We demonstrate the approach using laboratory experiments on contaminant transport through a multilayer porous column with an iodinated contrast medium. The model accurately reproduces breakthrough dynamics while revealing systematic parameter dependence on flow conditions. In particular, it identifies a nonlinear decrease in effective sorption capacity with increasing flow rate and porosity, consistent with kinetic limitations and reduced adsorbent mass. The Bayesian formulation also uncovers a strong negative correlation between sorption affinity and sorption capacity, quantifying the intrinsic non-identifiability of the inverse problem. CoDe-BPINN provides a robust framework for parameter inference and uncertainty quantification in data-scarce reactive transport problems.

</details>


### [166] [Investigation of the Asymptotic Properties of Active Impedance in Large Finite Array Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17844)
*Harald Hultin,Lucas Åkerstedt,B. L. G. Jonsson*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的有限阵列天线全波求解器，并利用其研究大型规则阵列有源阻抗的渐近特性。该求解器结合了适用于更广泛有限几何结构的预处理方案和改进的数据结构，具有更快、更稳定且内存占用更低的优点。研究表明，即使对于包含1000个单元的阵列，其有源阻抗的渐近行为仍可能与无限阵列不同。文中提出了多种预测中心单元有源阻抗行为的方法，其中两种最佳预测方法在所研究的宽带阵列中表现良好，可用于判断何时适用阵列近似方法（如单元法），而无需依赖关于阵列尺寸的一般性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地分析大型有限阵列天线的电磁特性，特别是有源阻抗的渐近行为，需要一种高效且稳定的全波求解器。传统方法在处理大规模有限阵列时存在计算效率低或内存消耗大的问题，且常依赖无限阵列假设，可能带来误差。因此，开发一种适用于一般有限几何结构的快速求解器，并探究有限阵列的真实渐近特性，具有重要意义。

Method: 本文采用了一种改进的全波求解器，核心在于引入了一种适应更广泛有限几何结构的预处理方案以及优化的数据结构。基于此求解器，对具有上千个单元的有限阵列进行仿真，系统分析其中心单元的有源阻抗随阵列尺寸变化的趋势，并与无限阵列结果对比，进而提出并验证多种用于预测有源阻抗行为的经验公式或判据。

Result: 改进后的求解器展现出更快的计算速度、更高的稳定性以及更低的内存占用。仿真结果显示，即使在包含1000个元件的有限阵列中，其有源阻抗的渐近行为仍显著不同于无限阵列模型的预测。文中提出的多个预测器中，有两个在宽带阵列中表现出优异的预测能力，能够有效判断何时可以使用如单元法等阵列近似方法。

Conclusion: 本文的改进全波求解器为分析大型有限阵列提供了高效工具。研究揭示了有限阵列有源阻抗的独特渐近特性，挑战了基于无限阵列假设的常规认知。所提出的高性能预测器有助于在实际工程设计中合理选择分析方法，避免不必要的简化或过度计算，提升了阵列天线设计的准确性与效率。

Abstract: This paper presents an improved full-wave solver for finite array antennas, and uses this solver to examine asymptotic properties of active impedance for large regular arrays. The improved solver is based on a preconditioning scheme that has been adapted for use on more general finite geometries, and an improved data structure. These two improvements result in a fast and stable solver with a lower memory footprint. By investigating the active impedance of finite arrays it is found that, even for arrays with 1000 elements, asymptotic behavior may differ from infinite arrays. Tied to these properties, different predictors on how the active impedance of the center element in the array behaves are presented. The two best predictors work very well for the wideband arrays investigated, and may be used to decide when array approximations, such as unit cell methods, are appropriate, rather than general statements on array size.

</details>

### [167] [Mutual Recognition in the Philosophy of Physics: QBism, Phenomenology, Hegel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17472)
*George Webster*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析QBism对维格纳朋友悖论的回应，揭示了其在本体论上对主体间相互承认的隐含承诺，并将这一观念与梅洛-庞蒂和黑格尔的哲学传统联系起来，以构建更坚实的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清QBism在代理性（agency）问题上的本体论立场，尤其是在面对量子力学解释中的主体角色时。

Method: 通过重构QBism对维格纳朋友悖论的两种回应方式，辨析其内在逻辑，并引入现象学与德国观念论资源进行哲学深化。

Result: 发现QBism蕴含一种相互承认的代理性概念，该概念依赖于主体之间的相互认可；并表明梅洛-庞蒂的主体间性与黑格尔的相互承认理论为此提供了哲学支持。

Conclusion: 将QBism置于相互承认的思想传统中，不仅阐明了其理论结构，还为其本体论提供了新的辩护路径与概念资源。

Abstract: Recent discussions of QBism have turned increasingly toward the question of its ontology. I argue that key insights into QBism's ontology of agency can be identified by reconstructing its response to Wigner's friend paradox. I clarify the relation between two formulations of that response: one grounded in the claim that quantum states are agents' personal probability assignments, and another grounded in the injunction to treat all users of quantum theory as agents on equal footing. Once disambiguated, these formulations reveal an implicit commitment to a mutually recognitive conception of agency, according to which the agential status of a user of quantum theory depends constitutively on the acknowledgement of other agents. This analysis clarifies what is at stake in recent QBist engagements with phenomenology - namely, the search for a more philosophically robust account of this conception of agency. I show how Merleau-Ponty's account of intersubjectivity underpins the recognitive structure implicated in QBism before tracing these themes to Hegel's account of mutual recognition. By locating QBism within a longstanding tradition of mutual recognition, I claim that we not only illuminate its theoretical structure but also open up novel conceptual resources for the rigorous articulation and justification of its ontology.

</details>


### [168] [Astrobiology and the Transformation of Scientific Epistemology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17728)
*Kristina Šekrst*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 天体生物学在缺乏直接经验证据的情况下，以地球生命为唯一参考，通过溯因推理探索地外生命，是一种‘过渡性科学’，其发展依赖于概念与实验工具的完善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于仅有地球这一个生命样本（n=1问题），天体生物学面临独特的认识论挑战，亟需在无确定性基础的情况下建立对非地球生命的理解框架。

Method: 采用溯因推理为主的方法，结合跨学科视角，将天体生物学置于哲学与新兴科学（如人工智能、认知科学）的交叉背景中进行分析。

Result: 提出天体生物学是一种‘过渡性科学’，其特征是缺乏证据、确定性和类比基础，科学进展体现为识别陌生生命形式的能力提升，而非传统意义上的假设验证。

Conclusion: 天体生物学代表了科学知识建构方式的转变，当研究对象超出直接经验范围时，科学实践转向工具与概念的发展，以应对长期不可观测或不可检验的假设。

Abstract: Astrobiology occupies an unusual position within the philosophy of science. Confronted with the n = 1 problem - having only a single example of life to study - it attempts to investigate life beyond Earth while relying entirely on Earth's biosphere as its reference point, a constraint that creates unique epistemic challenges. Unlike traditional sciences with clear predictive frameworks, astrobiology operates as what we might call a transient science: a discipline functioning without foundational certainties, relying predominantly on abductive reasoning, and confronting hypotheses that may remain untestable for decades. It is, in essence, a science of absence - of evidence, certainty, and analogy - where progress lies in refining conceptual and experimental tools to recognize unfamiliar forms of life. This positions astrobiology alongside emerging fields like artificial intelligence and cognitive science within a broader transformation of how scientific knowledge is constructed when dealing with phenomena that transcend direct empirical access.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Computing the Critical Temperature of the Affine-Transformed $D=3$ Ising Model Using Masked Autoregressive Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17006)
*Kai Svenson,George T. Fleming,Richard C. Brower,Nobuyuki Matsumoto,Rohan Misra*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了在面心立方晶格上仿射变换的Ising模型的临界温度测量方法，比较了传统的多直方图法与基于机器学习的掩码自回归流（MAF）方法，发现MAF在计算效率和可扩展性方面更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在任意弯曲流形上构建共形场论（CFT），需要精确且高效地确定晶格场论中的临界参数，如临界温度β_c，特别是在复杂晶格结构下的计算方法仍需改进。

Method: 采用多直方图（MH）方法与掩码自回归流（MAF）两种方法对蒙特卡洛观测值进行插值和外推，以估计仿射变换Ising模型在面心立方晶格上的临界温度β_c，并比较两者的计算效率与准确性。

Result: MAF方法虽然并非专为蒙特卡洛数据设计，但在测量β_c方面表现出与MH方法相当的精度，且其计算成本随系统规模增长更缓慢，具备更好的可扩展性，并能有效外推至不同系统体积。

Conclusion: MAF是一种可行且高效的替代方法，适用于复杂晶格系统中临界温度的测定，未来有望推广至更复杂的CFT构造任务中。

Abstract: The simple Ising model provides a rich environment to build and study lattice field theories. As part of an ongoing project to construct a conformal field theory (CFT) on an arbitrarily curved manifold, in this work we develop methods to measure the critical temperature $β_c$ of the affine-transformed Ising model on the face-centered cubic (FCC) lattice. The main challenge in this endeavor is finding a computationally efficient and accurate method of interpolating and extrapolating Monte Carlo observables with respect to coupling coefficients and temperature. Herein, we compare two such methods. A traditional statistical approach uses the multiple histogram (MH) method, while a newer machine learning approach uses a masked autoregressive flow (MAF) to estimate the underlying probability density function of a set of observables. While the MH method is specifically designed to interpolate and extrapolate Monte Carlo observables, we find that MAF is a viable alternative for measuring $β_c$ with a computational cost that scales more favorably. Furthermore, we comment on additional advantages of MAF relevant to our work, such as extrapolating in system volume.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Reduced-Order Physics-Informed Neural Network with Adaptive Basis Refinement for Structural Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17131)
*Rui Zhang,Konstantinos Vlachas,Eleni Chatzi*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于结构识别的降阶物理信息神经网络（RO-PINN）框架，结合自适应基底 refinement，可在已知或不完整物理条件下实现高效参数识别与非线性恢复力重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN在结构动力学中受限于高维和物理模型误差，而传统降阶模型多依赖固定子空间且难以用于逆问题识别，尤其在参数变化或物理知识不完整时表现受限。

Method: 提出RO-PINN框架，通过投影将降阶控制方程嵌入PINN损失函数，在低维潜在空间中学习；训练过程中采用自适应策略更新投影基底，以对齐演化中的结构参数或恢复力，减少基底失配误差。

Result: 在四层钢框架结构上验证方法，结果表明其在稀疏噪声数据下可准确识别参数，精度媲美或优于贝叶斯更新方法且计算成本更低；能恢复未建模的非线性恢复力，并在同一框架下联合识别参数与残余力。

Conclusion: RO-PINN为结构识别提供了一个统一框架，融合了降阶建模、自适应基底更新和物理信息学习，适用于不完整物理和参数变化场景，具有较高计算效率与识别精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a flexible framework for solving forward and inverse problems. However, their direct application to structural dynamics remains limited by high system dimensionality and model-form errors arising from incomplete physics. Reduced-order models (ROMs) can alleviate the dimensionality bottleneck, yet existing PINN-ROM couplings typically rely on fixed reduced subspaces, target forward simulations, or assume complete physics, restricting their use for inverse identification under parametric variability or incomplete system knowledge. To address these limitations, this work proposes a Reduced-Order Physics-Informed Neural Network (RO-PINN) framework with adaptive basis refinement for structural identification under known and incomplete physics. Via projection, reduced governing equations are embedded directly into the PINN loss, facilitating learning in a low-dimensional latent space. An adaptive scheme updates the projection basis during training so that the latent space is progressively realigned with evolving structural parameters or learned residual restoring forces. This realignment reduces basis-mismatch errors and limits their influence on the inferred residual force. The method is validated on a four-story steel frame with nonlinear hysteretic braces under sparse and noisy measurements. Results show parameter identification comparable to or more accurate than Bayesian model updating with lower computational cost in the considered cases, recovery of unmodeled nonlinear restoring forces under incomplete physics, and joint identification of residual restoring forces and structural parameters within the same framework. Overall, RO-PINN provides a unified framework for structural identification by integrating reduced-order modeling, adaptive basis refinement, and physics-informed learning within a single formulation.

</details>


### [171] [Modeling the Hydrodynamics in the Oslofjord using ADCIRC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17140)
*Matthew Scarborough,Kai Håkon Christensen,Albert Cerrone,Nils Melsom Kristensen,Eirik Valseth*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究利用OceanMesh2D基于全球水深和海岸线数据构建了奥斯陆峡湾的非结构化计算网格，并建立了ADCIRC模型。该模型在不同时间段进行了验证，结果表明其预测精度与当前挪威使用的预报模型相当，但计算时间显著减少。研究还评估了不同潮汐分量组合对模型性能的影响，并尝试使用全球ADCIRC模型的水位输出来改进边界条件，以提高极端天气事件下的模拟精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高奥斯陆峡湾水动力模拟的效率和精度，同时减少计算成本，研究旨在开发一种新的非结构化网格并评估其在区域模型中的应用潜力。

Method: 采用OceanMesh2D工具结合全球水深和海岸线数据生成包含70,410个节点的非结构化网格，分辨率在海岸线处为50米；基于该网格建立ADCIRC模型，进行多个时间段的模拟，并与现有模型及实测水位数据对比验证；测试三种不同潮汐分量组合的强迫效果，并引入全球ADCIRC模型的水位输出作为边界条件以改善极端事件下的模拟表现。

Result: 新模型在模拟精度上与当前挪威业务化模型相当，但计算耗时更少；增加潮汐分量可轻微提升结果，但受限于奥斯陆峡湾的小尺度域，潮汐边界条件的技能有限；使用全球ADCIRC模型提供的水位边界条件有助于减小极端天气期间与观测数据的偏差。

Conclusion: 所提出的非结构化网格和ADCIRC模型能够在保证模拟精度的同时显著降低计算成本，具备用于区域水动力预报的潜力；进一步改进边界条件（如引入大尺度模型输出）是提升局部模型在极端条件下表现的关键方向。

Abstract: This study introduces a new unstructured computational mesh for hydrodynamic simulations of the Oslofjord. The mesh was created with global bathymetry and shoreline data, using OceanMesh2D. It contains 70,410 nodes, with a resolution at the coastline of 50 meters. We use the new mesh to create an ADCIRC model of the fjord. The model is run for four time periods with different characteristics, and validated against the current state of the art and elevation gauges in the fjord. Results show that the model achieves similar results to the model currently used for forecasting in Norway, while requiring much less computation time. Three different combinations of tidal constituents are used to force the model, and analyze the cost and benefits of using additional constituents, finding that they slightly improve results. However, the skill of the tidal forcing boundary condition is limited, because of the small domain of the Oslofjord. In order to further reconcile the results' deviation from the gauge data, especially during extreme weather events, the water surface elevation output from a global ADCIRC model was used to force the model instead of tides.

</details>


### [172] [Information fusion and machine learning for sensitivity analysis using physics knowledge and experimental data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17248)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Sankaran Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在全局敏感性分析中，如何利用物理信息机器学习方法结合物理模型与实验观测数据，以提高敏感性估计的准确性。采用深度神经网络（DNN）和高斯过程（GP）两种机器学习技术，并通过引入物理约束损失函数或分阶段训练策略融合物理知识。结果表明，DNN模型在敏感性估计的不确定性边界方面优于GP模型，方法在增材制造和湖泊温度建模实例中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升工程系统敏感性分析中估计结果的准确性，需有效融合物理模型与实验数据，克服单一模型因精度和不确定性带来的局限。

Method: 采用深度神经网络（DNN）和高斯过程（GP）两种机器学习方法，结合两种物理信息融合策略：一是引入物理约束的损失函数；二是先用仿真数据预训练、再用实验数据更新模型。构建四类模型，并在Sobol指数计算中考虑模型不确定性。

Result: DNN模型由于参数自由度高、训练灵活，相比GP模型能获得更小的敏感性估计不确定性边界。

Conclusion: 物理信息机器学习能有效融合物理模型与实验数据，提升全局敏感性分析的准确性，其中DNN结构更具优势。

Abstract: When computational models (either physics-based or data-driven) are used for the sensitivity analysis of engineering systems, the sensitivity estimate is affected by the accuracy and uncertainty of the model. This paper considers global sensitivity analysis (GSA) for situations where both a physics-based model and experimental observations are available, and investigates physics-informed machine learning strategies to effectively combine the two sources of information in order to maximize the accuracy of the sensitivity estimate. Two representative machine learning (ML) techniques are considered, namely, deep neural networks (DNN) and Gaussian process (GP) modeling, and two strategies for incorporating physics knowledge within these techniques are investigated, namely: (i) incorporating loss functions in the ML models to enforce physics constraints, and (ii) pre-training and updating the ML model using simulation and experimental data respectively. Four different models are built for each type (DNN and GP), and the uncertainties in these models are included in the Sobol indices computation. The DNN-based models, with many degrees of freedom in terms of model parameters and training options, are found to result in smaller bounds on the sensitivity estimates when compared to the GP-based models. The proposed methods are illustrated for additive manufacturing and lake temperature modeling examples.

</details>


### [173] [Adaptive surrogate modeling for high-dimensional spatio-temporal output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17250)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Shunsaku Matsumoto,Yoshitomo Miyagi,Daigo Watanabe,Sankaran Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应代理建模方法，用于处理高维时空输出问题，通过降维和主动采样提高模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时空输出使得多物理场系统分析计算成本高昂，传统代理模型难以高效处理。

Method: 首先采用降维方法将高维输出映射到低维潜在空间，然后在低维空间构建代理模型，并基于预测误差进行自适应采样以优化模型。

Result: 在燃气涡轮发动机叶片的热力分析中验证了该方法的有效性，能够在较少仿真次数下显著提升代理模型精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应代理建模方法能有效应对高维时空输出的挑战，显著提高计算效率和模型准确性。

Abstract: This paper develops an adaptive surrogate modeling method for problems with very high-dimensional spatio-temporal outputs. The analysis of spatio-temporal multi-physics systems is computationally expensive and consists of a large number of inputs and outputs. Surrogate models are often constructed to replace the physics-based model to achieve computational efficiency in analyses such as uncertainty quantification and optimization that require many function calls. In order to address the challenge introduced by the high dimensionality of spatio-temporal output, a dimension reduction method is first employed to map the high-dimensional output to a low-dimensional latent space. This is followed by the construction of the surrogate model in the low-dimensional space. The prediction error in the original space, which includes both the reconstruction error and surrogate model error, is evaluated using different error metrics. Based on the prediction accuracy of the surrogate model, new training points are identified for adaptive improvement of the surrogate model. We present a novel adaptive sampling technique that combines exploration and exploitation to improve the surrogate model accuracy with the fewest possible runs of the expensive physics-based model. Thermo-mechanical analysis of a gas turbine engine blade is used to analyze the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [174] [A multi-level preprocessing and modelling framework for spectral imaging of microplastics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17697)
*Zina-Sabrina Duma,Tenzin Tsering,Sara Heikkinen,Tuomo Soininen,Tuomas Sihvonen,Arto Koistinen,Satu-Pia Reinikainen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种用于微塑料FT-IR光谱成像的多级预处理与建模框架，结合图像、图块和光谱层级的校正方法，并通过聚类中心光谱匹配提升识别效率与准确性，显著提高了微塑料识别的鲁棒性、计算效率和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于数据量大、采集伪影、光谱变异性和相似光谱导致的误识别，微塑料的常规光谱成像应用受到限制，因此需要更稳健、高效的识别方法。

Method: 采用多级预处理策略：在图像级使用潜在变量选择消除采集条件变化的影响；在图块级进行基于背景的照明伪影校正；在光谱级进行基线校正、平滑、导数计算、归一化和波长选择，并仅保留颗粒光谱以提高效率；通过聚类分析对颗粒光谱进行分组，并在聚类中心进行光谱库匹配，而非逐像素匹配。

Result: 在12种匹配策略中，基于符号不变导数的余弦相似性方法对PS、PET、PE和PP实现了100%的分类准确率；聚类方法生成的空间粒子图比直接软件匹配更连贯，且大幅缩短了处理时间。

Conclusion: 多级校正结合聚类中心光谱匹配的框架显著提升了光谱成像技术在微塑料识别中的鲁棒性、效率和结果可解释性，有助于推动该技术的常规化应用。

Abstract: Spectral imaging provides chemically specific and spatially resolved analysis of microplastics, but its routine application is hindered by large data volumes, acquisition artefacts, spectral variability, and misidentification of polymers due to alike spectra. This study proposes a multi-level preprocessing and modelling framework for FT-IR spectral imaging of microplastics that integrates image-level, tile-level, and spectral-level corrections with scalable identification strategies.
  Image-level variation associated with changing acquisition conditions was done with latent variable selection, while a background-based tile correction reduced illumination-related artefacts. Spectral preprocessing combined baseline correction, smoothing, derivative calculation, normalization, and wavelength selection, and only particle spectra were retained for further analysis to improve computational efficiency. For scalable identification, clustering was applied to particle spectra and spectral library matching was performed on cluster centroids instead of individual pixels. Among twelve evaluated matching strategies, a sign-invariant derivative-based cosine similarity method achieved perfect classification accuracy for polystyrene (PS), polyethylene terephthalate (PET), polyethylene (PE), and polypropylene (PP). The clustering-based workflow also produced more spatially coherent particle maps than direct software-based matching while substantially reducing processing time. The framework was evaluated for supervised classification-based MP indentification. These results show that multi-level correction combined with cluster-centroid spectral matching improves the robustness, efficiency, and interpretability of spectral-imaging-based microplastic identification.

</details>


### [175] [Overlap-free multi-material topology optimization for minimum compliance in two and three dimensions by level-set-based negative-mapping interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17963)
*Dong Wang,Qianglin Ran,Xuanliang Wang,Wei Xiang,Wenming Cheng,Run Du*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于水平集的负映射插值方法，用于宏观结构的多材料比例拓扑优化，有效消除灰度元素和材料重叠问题，并在多个二维和三维算例中表现出与经典SIMP方法相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决多材料拓扑优化中存在的灰度元素和材料界面重叠问题，提升结构设计的清晰度和实用性。

Method: 采用交替活性相算法将多相问题分解为双相子问题，结合水平集函数描述，并引入进化策略避免复杂的灵敏度计算，通过负映射插值消除材料重叠。

Result: 在2D悬臂梁、MBB梁和3D悬臂梁的数值实验中，该方法消除了灰度元素，生成了清晰平滑的边界，实现了无重叠的材料分布，且柔顺性与SIMP方法相当，在8个二维案例中有4个优于SIMP方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效实现高质量的多材料拓扑优化，具有良好的应用前景，尤其适用于需要清晰材料界面和高性能结构的设计场景。

Abstract: To address challenges such as gray elements and material overlaps, this paper extends the level set-based negative-mapping interpolation method to the multi-material proportional topology optimization of macro-scale structures in two and three dimensions. The approach utilizes an alternating active-phase algorithm to decompose M-phase problems into simplified two-phase subproblems described by level set functions. By integrating an evolutionary strategy, the method circumvents complex sensitivity calculations. A negative-mapping interpolation then removes the material overlaps at the interfaces. Numerical experiments on 2D cantilever and MBB beams and on a 3D cantilever beam demonstrate that the present method eradicates gray elements, produces smooth boundaries and ensures overlap-free material distributions at a compliance comparable to that of the classical SIMP method, lower than the SIMP value in four of the eight two-dimensional test cases and higher by 0.3%, 0.4%, 4.8% and 12.7% in the other four; the influence of the material properties, of the interface treatment and of the number of iterations on the results is also discussed.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Adaptive Participation Under Statically Equivalent Incentives in Distributed Demand Response Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17469)
*Xun Shao,Ryoichi Inoue,Shinken Takekawa,Go Hasegawa*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文研究了在分布式能源资源聚合机制中，静态均衡检验无法预测动态参与行为的问题。实验表明，两种满足相同静态标准的支付衰减结构，在动态学习环境下导致完全不同的参与结果：一种实现了稳定全参与，另一种则完全无法恢复。差异源于部分参与时的经验反馈，而提供反事实收益信息可消除此差异。此外，机制在分布式实现下仍能准确复现集中式结果，并具备声明溯源能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚合机制设计通常只验证固定点上的均衡性质（如零参与非均衡、真实申报为最佳回应），但这些静态检验是否足以预测资源所有者在适应过程中的实际参与水平尚不明确。作者希望探究动态调整过程如何影响最终参与状态。

Method: 在一个五单元固定调度、支付规则和惩罚的实验环境中，仅改变稀缺性依赖的参与支付随他人申报能力的衰减方式。比较两种在五个静态标准上一致但动态表现迥异的衰减结构，通过多轮模拟观察从崩溃初始化出发的参与演化路径，并引入反事实收益与分布式实现进行对比分析。

Result: 尽管两种支付结构在静态检验上表现一致，但在8000轮内，一种结构在96次独立运行中均成功恢复全参与，另一种则无一次成功，且置信区间无重叠。差异源自部分参与阶段的收益反馈，静态标准未覆盖该情况；当提供反事实收益后，两者表现趋同。分布式实现能精确复现集中式结果，且可检测错误归属的申报。

Conclusion: 静态均衡检验不足以保证良好的动态参与性能，机制设计应考虑适应过程中的经验反馈。提供反事实信息可改善学习效果，且该机制具备向分布式可靠扩展的能力。

Abstract: Aggregators recruit distributed energy resources with settlement rules and participation payments. Such designs are normally validated at fixed points: zero participation must cease to be an equilibrium, and truthful capability reporting must remain a best reply. We ask whether those checks determine the participation that owners reach once they adapt from the settlements they receive. In a five-unit event with fixed dispatch, payment rule and penalty, we vary only how a scarcity-contingent participation payment decays with the capability others have declared. Of two decay structures that agree on all five static criteria, one reaches full participation from a collapse initialization in 96 of 96 seeds and the other in none, within an 8000-round horizon and with disjoint 95% confidence intervals. The difference lies in the payoffs offered at partial participation, which the static criteria never evaluate; it is a property of experience-based feedback and closes when counterfactual payoffs are supplied. Because those payoffs make each unit's settlement depend on what the others declared, we also ask what survives when the mechanism is distributed. Running the aggregator and the five units as separate processes reproduced the centralized reference at every round, and a deliberately misattributed declaration was detected although every message was delivered.

</details>


<div id='physics.ao-ph'></div>

# physics.ao-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Anthropogenic Heat in Urban Climate Systems: Forcing, Sensitivity, and Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17782)
*Dan Li,Alvin Christopher Galang Varquez,Ting Sun,Yuya Takane,Jiachuan Yang,Mingze Ding,David Sailor*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了人为热通量在城市气候系统中的作用，提出了一个强迫-响应-反馈框架，以整合当前的理解，并确定未来研究的优先方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人为热通量是城市气候系统的关键特征，对城市热岛效应和气象过程有重要影响，但其在城市能量平衡中的量化仍不充分，且缺乏普适性的物理理解。

Method: 通过文献综述，提出一个“强迫-响应-反馈”分析框架，系统梳理现有研究成果。

Result: 明确了人为热通量作为城市气候强迫因子的作用机制及其与城市温度响应和反馈过程的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于深化对人为热通量气候意义的物理理解，并为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Anthropogenic heat flux, the heat released to the environment from human activities such as building energy use, transportation, industrial processes, and human metabolism, is a defining feature of the urban climate system. It is an important contributor to the urban heat island (UHI) effect and influences a wide range of urban meteorological processes. Its significance extends beyond urban climatology because of its close connections to energy consumption, greenhouse gas emissions, and climate adaptation. Despite decades of research, anthropogenic heat flux remains one of the least well-constrained components of the urban energy balance. Moreover, its climatic significance has often been assessed from an applied perspective, with less emphasis on developing transferable physical understanding of how anthropogenic heat flux acts as a forcing, how urban temperatures respond, and how feedbacks modify that response. This review develops a forcing-response-feedback framework for synthesizing current understanding of the role of anthropogenic heat flux in the urban climate system and identifies priorities for future research.

</details>


### [178] [How Do AI Climate Models Respond to Warming Across Climate Zones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17986)
*Charlotte C. Merchant,Milan Klöwer,Bradley Stanley-Clamp,Maren Höver,Simon L. L. Michel,Edward Groot,Hannah M. Christensen*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: AI气候模型在模拟气候区变化时的物理一致性存在差异，仅混合物理-AI模型NeuralGCM-HRD符合热力学和水文尺度关系，其他模型在陆地单元处理上存在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI气候模型在变暖情景下是否以符合物理规律的方式泛化气候区分布，以判断其用于气候预测的适用性。

Method: 采用柯本-盖格气候区分类方法，分析AIMIP第1阶段模型在+4K海温强迫下的响应，并与基于物理的AMIP模型进行比较，评估温度、降水和近地面比湿的变化及空间结构差异。

Result: 所有AI模型都能在1979–2014年ERA5气候态范围内再现基准分类精度，但只有NeuralGCM-HRD模型的气候区重组符合已知的热力学和水文尺度关系，其余模型因对陆地网格的架构处理不当而出现明显失败模式。

Conclusion: 用于气候预测的AI模型必须具备物理一致的气候区响应能力，当前多数纯AI模型在此方面仍存在不足。

Abstract: Regional climate zones are expected to shift under global warming. Whether AI climate models have learned to generalize climate-zone distributions under warming in a physically meaningful way affects their suitability for climate projection. We address this question by applying a Köppen-Geiger climate-zone decomposition to AIMIP Phase 1 models under prescribed +4K SST forcing and comparing their responses to physics-based AMIP models. Using this diagnostic, we compare baseline classification skill, per-zone responses in temperature, precipitation, and near-surface specific humidity, and the spatial structure of departures from physics-based models. All AI models considered reproduce the 1979-2014 ERA5 climatology within the physics-based models' range, but only the hybrid physics-AI model NeuralGCM-HRD reorganizes zones in agreement with established thermodynamic and hydrological scaling relations. The remaining emulators have distinct failure modes traceable to their architectural treatment of land cells. A physically consistent climate-zone response is therefore necessary for AI models intended for climate projection.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.CP'></div>

# q-fin.CP [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [COS-TT-CHF: A Tensor-Train Characteristic-Function COS Method for Multi-Asset Option Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17636)
*Lucas Arenstein,Michael Kastoryano*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量训练（tensor-train, TT）压缩的COS-TT-CHF方法，用于在Lévy和仿射特征函数模型下高效定价高维欧式多资产期权，有效缓解了维度灾难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多维COS方法因张量积结构导致系数数组规模随资产数量指数增长，难以应用于高维期权定价，本文旨在通过低秩张量压缩技术克服这一计算障碍。

Method: 提出并扩展了COS-TT-CHF方法，利用TT-cross算法将采样的特征函数张量压缩为张量训练格式的COS系数，并应用于算术篮子期权和min/max期权的定价，支持快速的后续执行价网格及敏感性计算。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在d=2到d=4时相对于直接COS方法具有计算优势，在d≥3时优于张量傅里叶min-option基准，且在d=2时已优于基于Sobol序列的拟蒙特卡罗方法；测试案例最高达到d=30（GBM）和d=20（VG、NIG、common-Heston），并报告了精度、秩、运行时间及Delta/Vega敏感性结果。

Conclusion: COS-TT-CHF方法通过张量压缩显著提升了高维期权定价的效率与可行性，为多资产衍生品在复杂模型下的快速风险计量提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper considers European multi-asset option pricing under Lévy and affine characteristic-function models. The main obstruction is the curse of dimensionality: direct multidimensional COS pricing forms tensor-product coefficient arrays whose size grows exponentially with the number of assets. We study and extend COS-TT-CHF, a low-rank construction that uses TT-cross to compress sampled characteristic-function tensors into tensor-train COS coefficients for arithmetic basket and min/max option pricing. Once built, the compressed representation gives fast post-setup strike-grid and selected component Delta/Vega calculations. The numerical study compares with adaptive-quadrature Fourier benchmarks, direct COS, a tensor-Fourier min-option benchmark, and quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC) references based on randomized Sobol points. The reported timings show a low-dimensional crossover against direct COS as the benchmark moves from $d=2$ to $d=4$, favorable timings against the tensor-Fourier min-option benchmark from $d=3$ onward, and favorable timings against the QMC common-Heston reference already at $d=2$. The reported tests reach $d=30$ for GBM and $d=20$ for VG, NIG, and common-Heston benchmark families, with accuracy, rank, runtime, control-sensitivity, and component Delta/Vega diagnostics reported throughout.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [CityReal: Human-Aligned Urban Behavior and City Dynamics Simulation with Large-Scale LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16897)
*Nicolas Bougie,Xiaotong Ye,Narimasa Watanabe*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: CityReal是一个模块化框架，通过意图驱动的决策机制和文本适配器实现与真实人群行为对齐的大规模城市模拟，在微观和宏观层面均表现出更高的现实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的城市模拟方法通常依赖少样本提示，导致智能体复制模型先验行为而非目标人群的真实行为，缺乏人口级真实性。

Method: 提出CityReal框架，将智能体建模为追求连贯活动与移动计划的意图驱动决策者，并通过学习习惯和偏好动态适应；利用文本适配器调整行为模块，使其决策与观测到的人口统计特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明CityReal在微观个体行为和宏观群体模式上均更贴近真实人类行为，可扩展至数万个智能体，支持人群密度、场所热度、流动模式及不同城市场景下的福祉分析。

Conclusion: CityReal提供了一个可扩展的城市模拟测试平台，显著提升了模拟结果在个体与群体层面的真实性，有助于城市规划与政策预测。

Abstract: Large-scale urban simulation plays a pivotal role in social science, traffic safety, and transportation policy. Recent work has shown that large language models, when prompted as agents, can generate lifelike daily routines at city scale. Yet these methods typically rely on few-shot prompting, causing agents to reproduce the LLM's behavioral priors rather than the target population. We introduce CityReal, a modular framework for human-aligned urban simulation. CityReal models agents as intention-driven decision makers that pursue coherent mobility and activity plans rather than isolated step-by-step choices. They adapt over time by learning habits and preferences based on experience and constraints. To improve population-level realism, we learn textual adapters for behavior modules that align agent decisions with observed population statistics. Experiments show that CityReal improves alignment with real-world human behavior at both micro and macro levels. Scaling to tens of thousands of agents, it supports analysis of crowd density, place popularity, mobility flows, and well-being under different urban scenarios, offering a scalable testbed for urban simulation and forecasting.

</details>


### [181] [QuantumNovelty: A Skill-Orchestrating Language Agent for Referee-Style Review and Patentability Screening of Quantum Papers and Patents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16900)
*Shlomo Kashani*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: QuantumNovelty是一个开源的语言代理，能够生成和审查量子计算领域的研究成果，通过可审计、可重复和成本透明的方式，利用确定性验证机制检测并防止夸大主张，辅助而非替代同行评审或专利审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型代理是否能以可审计、可重复且成本透明的方式审查其自身生成的量子科学成果，提升科研产出的可信度与透明度。

Method: 设计了一个名为QuantumNovelty的技能协调型语言代理，集成生成与审查功能，并引入‘审计与证伪’层，包含严格帕累托支配、数值重计算、威尔逊小样本区间和跨供应商共识保护等确定性验证机制，所有模型调用均记录后端、令牌数和成本。

Result: 在植入对抗性语料库中，确定性机制成功捕获所有人为夸大声明且无假阳性；在首次部署中（六篇手稿和一项已授权专利，成本约24美元），审查小组的判断比公开接受记录更为保守；所有过程可审计，部分机制在实际输入中尚未完全启用。

Conclusion: QuantumNovelty提供了一种可审计、低成本的决策支持框架，可用于辅助科研与专利审查流程，但不取代人类专家评审，强调透明性与可验证性，同时坦陈当前机制的局限性。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly produce quantum-science results; we ask whether the same agentic paradigm can also scrutinize them in an auditable, reproducible, and cost-transparent form. We present QuantumNovelty, an open-source skill-orchestrating language agent that both generates quantum-computing artifacts (papers, Pareto-front ansatz candidates, and patent drafts) and reviews them through simulated referee and patent-examiner panels. Its design contribution is an audit-and-falsify layer of deterministic gates -- strict Pareto domination, numerical recomputation from on-disk artifacts, Wilson small-sample intervals, and a cross-vendor consensus guard -- that constrains, rather than generates, the claims allowed to survive; every model call is logged with backend, token count, and cost. We make no accuracy claim against human experts, and validate only what is checkable without human labels: on a planted adversarial corpus the deterministic gates catch every planted overclaim with no false positives, and on a first deployment (six manuscripts and one granted patent, at a measured cost of about twenty-four US dollars) the panels are directionally more conservative than the public acceptance record, on a one-sided sample. The framework is decision support, not a replacement for peer review or patent examination, and we report in full where its mechanisms remain unexercised on real inputs.

</details>


### [182] [Reputation and institutional certification as complementary trust mechanisms in a single online market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17312)
*Yuta Kido,Yohsuke Ohtsubo*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在信息不对称条件下声誉与制度认证两种信任机制的交互作用，利用eBay上近百万条宝可梦卡牌交易数据，发现自我评级和第三方评级在不同声誉-价值区间内占据主导地位，且随着卖家声誉提高，自我评级的优势区域扩大。声誉增强了自我评级的价格溢价，但不影响第三方评级的溢价。作者构建了一个信号博弈模型，解释了这两种机制在交易内为替代关系、在市场上为互补关系，提出了由声誉和价值共同决定的信任机制 regime 结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解声誉与制度认证在信息不对称市场中的互动关系，尤其是二者是替代还是互补，现有理论尚不清晰，本文旨在揭示其内在机制与边界条件。

Method: 结合大规模实证分析与理论建模：使用eBay平台上近一百万条Pokemon卡牌固定价格交易数据，分析卖家采用第三方评级、自我评级或无信号的选择及其对价格的影响；构建一个信号博弈模型，引入基于声誉的虚假声明事后成本与认证的事前成本，解释观察到的模式。

Result: 1）自我评级与第三方评级在声誉-价值空间中占据不同的均衡区域，高声誉卖家更倾向于使用自我评级并获得更高溢价；2）声誉显著增强自我评级的价格溢价，但对第三方评级无此效应；3）模型显示虚假自评的成本与声誉正相关，而认证成本与声誉无关，从而形成不同均衡 regime；4）两种机制在个体交易中为替代关系，在整体市场中为互补关系。

Conclusion: 声誉与制度认证并非简单的对立关系，而是根据卖家声誉与商品价值形成动态互补与替代的 regime 结构，高声誉降低了对正式认证的依赖，使自我声明更具可信度，从而重塑了信任机制的设计逻辑。

Abstract: Reputation and institutional certification are the two main trust mechanisms under information asymmetry, yet their interaction remains poorly understood. We analyze nearly one million fixed-price eBay listings of Pokemon cards, where sellers choose among three signals: third-party grading (institutional certification), self-grading (a self-claimed condition description), or no signal. We show that self-grading and third-party grading dominate distinct regions of the reputation-value space, with the self-grading region widening as reputation rises. Reputation also amplifies the price premium of self-grading but not that of third-party grading. We develop a signaling game in which a false self-claim carries an ex-post cost proportional to reputation, whereas certification carries an ex-ante cost independent of reputation. These costs split the reputation-value space into equilibrium regimes that explain both patterns. The two mechanisms are therefore substitutes within a transaction yet complements across the market, replacing the reputation-versus-institution dichotomy with a regime structure set by reputation and value.

</details>


### [183] [Spatially explicit feature importance for building height estimation using research-access high-resolution SAR and optical sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17822)
*Guilherme Iablonovski,Pierre-Louis Frison,Tatiana Silva da Silva*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究结合TerraSAR-X、PlanetScope和Sentinel-1等免费卫星数据，采用地理加权随机森林模型，在巴西某大城市实现了个体建筑高度的高精度估算，RMSE为5.34米，R²达0.756，揭示了不同城市环境中各遥感特征的重要性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏机载LiDAR和高分辨率商业影像的全球南方城市，难以获取城市尺度的个体建筑高度信息，而现有基于免费Sentinel影像的方法分辨率不足，无法满足材料存量分析等需求。因此，亟需利用可及性强且成本低的数据源提升建筑高度反演精度。

Method: 融合TerraSAR-X StripMap、PlanetScope和Sentinel-1三种自由获取的遥感数据，提取多源特征，并采用考虑空间自相关的地理加权随机森林模型进行建筑高度预测，通过与LiDAR参考数据对比评估模型性能。

Result: 模型取得5.34米的均方根误差（RMSE）和0.756的决定系数（R²）；局部特征重要性分析显示，低层建筑以轮廓几何特征为主导，较高孤立建筑以阴影高度为主，高层建筑则依赖光谱反射率；Sentinel-1后向散射与InSAR在不同区域具有互补作用，无单一传感器始终占优。

Conclusion: 多源免费卫星数据结合地理加权随机森林模型可在数据受限地区有效估计城市建筑高度，且能揭示不同城市环境下的特征主导模式，为全球尺度的机器学习模型提供可解释性与情境化指导。

Abstract: Accurate building height information at the individual footprint scale is essential for material stock accounting and post-disaster damage assessments yet remains difficult to obtain at city scale in the Global South where airborne LiDAR coverage is rare and commercial very high-resolution imagery is cost-prohibitive or unavailable. While recent works have demonstrated building height estimation using freely available Sentinel imagery, the resolution ceiling of resulting products is still coarse for material stock analysis. This study incorporates products derived from data freely accessible under scientific research licenses, TerraSAR-X StripMap and PlanetScope, alongside Sentinel-1 to predict building heights in a large city in Brazil. To account for the spatial autocorrelation in the training set, features from all sources are integrated in a geographically weighted random forest model, returning an RMSE of 5.34 m and R2 of 0.756 against a LiDAR reference dataset. Local feature importance showed predictor dominance to vary consistently across intra-urban contexts, with footprint geometry dominating for low-rise buildings, shadow-derived height for taller and more isolated structures, and spectral reflectance for the tallest buildings in the set. Sentinel-1 backscatter and InSAR occupy complementary spatial niches, with no single sensor uniformly preferable across the set. Results provide optioneering guidance and insight over satellite-derived products predictive relevance in distinct contexts, which global machine learning or neural network models cannot offer.

</details>


### [184] [Computational Prosopography across a Millennium: Mathematically Oriented Lineages Traced from the Fields Medalists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17915)
*Hiroyuki Chuma,Kanji Otsuka,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 通过Wikidata构建了跨越九个世纪的师生学术传承网络，利用64位菲尔兹奖得主作为追踪集，揭示出莱布尼茨在学术谱系中的“沙漏”结构，并发现上游54条谱系汇聚于五位中世纪学者，提出需结合工具批判来评估此类历史网络分析的测量偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨学术知识传承的历史脉络，理解现代学术体系的根源及其结构性特征，并评估基于大规模数据重构历史网络时的方法学偏见。

Method: 从Wikidata中提取约47万条导师-学生关系，构建包含372,853人的有向无环图；以64位菲尔兹奖得主为起点进行逆向遍历，生成2550万条路径；使用代数可逆的遍历引擎分析网络结构，并量化其测量偏差。

Result: 发现莱布尼茨处于一个‘沙漏’状结构的瓶颈，上游平均5.3条路径/节点，下游达53.4条，比例接近10:1，而牛顿仅出现在4条谱系中；七个独立维度在莱布尼茨时期发生重组，学会成员比例显著上升；54条谱系向上追溯至12-13世纪的五位伊斯兰与拜占庭学者，并止于11世纪的‘修道院之墙’边界。

Conclusion: 历史学术网络存在显著结构性特征，如沙漏瓶颈与上游收敛现象，这些发现需结合对重建工具本身的批判性分析才能准确解读，且主要结构在消除测量偏差后依然稳健。

Abstract: We reconstruct the mentor--student network through which documented scholarly training passed across roughly nine centuries, and subject both the network and the means of reconstructing it to source criticism. From Wikidata, which aggregates the Mathematics Genealogy Project and the MacTutor Archive, we extract approximately 470,000 mentor--student assertions, yielding a directed acyclic graph of 372,853 persons. Using all 64 historical Fields Medalists as a fixed, ex ante tracer set, backward traversal enumerates some 25.5 million distinct paths reaching 57 generations.
  Three structural observations follow. Genealogical traffic through Leibniz forms an hourglass: thin upstream, 5.3 paths per node on average, and thick downstream, 53.4, a ratio near 10:1, with no counterpart at Newton, who lies on only four of the 64 lineages. Across a window centered on Leibniz, seven independently extracted predicate dimensions reorganize together, and recorded learned-society membership rises from 6.5 to 82.1 percent of the cohort. Upstream, 54 of the 64 lineages converge on the same five twelfth- and thirteenth-century Islamic and Byzantine scholars before terminating at an eleventh-century boundary we name the Monastery Wall.
  We argue that such observations cannot be assessed without tool criticism. The traversal engine is algebraically reversible, so every ranking decision it makes can be reconstructed afterward. We characterize its measurement bias in closed form, show that the macro-structures survive switching that bias off, and report the family of lineages the traversal returns at different resolutions rather than a single ranked list.

</details>


### [185] [Simplicial closure fragments the explosive cooperation transitions in higher-order public goods games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17968)
*Wenjia Rao,Jianneng Huang*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 在高阶公共物品博弈中，单纯复形闭合对合作转变具有选择性影响：连续型转变时主要拓宽过渡区域，而爆发型转变时会将双稳态分裂为广泛的亚稳态最终状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超图与单纯复形在高阶网络合作动力学中的差异，尤其是在相似群体互动下两者对合作演化的影响是否仅存在定量区别。

Method: 通过控制参数最少的高阶公共物品博弈模型，比较基于相同三角骨干随机生成的超图与单纯复形上的合作相变行为。

Result: 发现单纯复形闭合的选择性作用：在连续型合作转变中主要拓宽过渡区，在爆发型转变中则将紧凑的高低双稳态分裂为大量亚稳态；该效应源于单纯复形闭合在促进局部合作核心存活的同时抑制其发展为全局级联。实验表明此分裂现象不依赖特定收益形式，是高阶公共物品博弈中爆发性合作转变的普遍特征。

Conclusion: 超图与单纯复形虽常被视为可互换的高阶网络表示，但在合作演化动力学中可能引发质的差异，单纯复形闭合能显著改变相变性质，尤其在爆发性转变中导致状态碎片化。

Abstract: Hypergraph (HG) and simplicial complex (SC) are two common representations of higher-order networks, and are often expected to differ mainly quantitatively when they encode comparable group interactions. Here we show that this expectation fails in evolutionary cooperation dynamics. Using a controlled higher-order public goods game (PGG) with minimal ad hoc parameters, we compare cooperation transitions on randomized HG and SC constructed from the same triangular backbone. We find that the impact of simplicial closure is selective: when the cooperation transition is continuous-like on HG, imposing simplicial closure mainly broadens the transition without changing its qualitative nature; however, when the transition is explosive and first-order-like on HG, simplicial closure will fragment the compact low/high bistability into a broad ensemble of metastable final states. Detailed analysis reveals that this selective effect arises from the dual role of simplicial closure in cooperative-nucleus dynamics: it promotes the survival of local cooperative nuclei while suppressing their conversion into system-wide cascades. Further experiments confirm that this fragmentation is not tied to a specific payoff form, but is a generic feature of explosive cooperation transitions in higher-order PGGs.

</details>
